procedure TfMain.CreateParams(var Params: TCreateParams);
begin
  inherited CreateParams(Params);
  with Params do begin
    ExStyle := ExStyle or WS_EX_APPWINDOW;
    WndParent := GetDesktopwindow;
  end;
end;

function ByFirst(Item1, Item2: TListItem; Data: integer):
  integer; stdcall;
var
  n1, n2:  cardinal;
begin
  n1 := StrToInt(Item1.Caption);
  n2 := StrToInt(Item2.Caption);
  if n1 > n2 then
    Result := 1       //1
  else if n1 < n2 then
    Result := 0        //-1
  else
    Result := -1;       //0
end;

function foundChar(Str: String;Chr: Char): Boolean;
Var
   i: integer;
begin
  result := false;
  for i := 1 to length(str) do
    if Str[i] = Chr then
      result := true;
end;

function foundAnyStr(Str: String): Boolean;
Var
   i: integer;
begin
  result := false;
  for i := 1 to length(str) do
    if Str[i] in ['-','+','/','.','*']  then
      result := true;
end;

function PosChar(Str: String;Chr: Char): Integer;
Var
   i: integer;
begin
  result := 0;
  i := 0;
  While Not (Str[i] = Chr) do
  begin
    inc(i);
  end;
  result := i;
end;

procedure ClearSumGrid(SGrid: TNiceGrid);
Var i : integer;
begin
  SGrid.BeginUpdate;
  for i := 0 to SGrid.RowCount-1 do
  begin
    SGrid[0,i] := '0';
    SGrid[1,i] := '0';
  end;
  Sgrid.Columns[0].Footer := '0';
  Sgrid.Columns[1].Footer := '0';
  SGrid.EndUpdate;
end;

procedure SumsGrid(SGrid: TNiceGrid);
Var i : integer;
    SumMoney,SumCom: Real;
begin
  SumMoney := 0;
  SumCom   := 0;

  Sgrid.BeginUpdate;
  for i := 0 to SGrid.RowCount-1 do
  begin
    SumMoney   := SumMoney + txttoFloat(SGrid[0,i]);
    SGrid[1,i] := FormatFloat('##,##0.##',txtToFloat(SGrid[0,i]) * TxtToFloat(SGrid[2,i]) / 100);
    SumCom     := SumCom   + txttoFloat(SGrid[1,i]);
  end;  
  Sgrid.Columns[0].Footer := FormatFloat('#,##0.##',SumMoney);
  Sgrid.Columns[1].Footer := FormatFloat('#,##0.##',SumCom);
  SGrid.EndUpdate;
end;

procedure TfMain.ShowCorNum;
var QrFindCorNum : TABSQuery;
begin
 with Dm do
  begin
    try
      QrFindCorNum := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
      QrFindCorNum.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
      QrFindCorNum.Close;
      QrFindCorNum.SQL.Clear;
      QrFindCorNum.SQL.Add('Select * from CorrectNum');
      QrFindCorNum.SQL.Add('where (CrDate = :aCrDate) and (LotType = :aLotType)');

      QrFindCorNum.ParamByName('aCrDate').Value  := DateTostr(DatePick.Date);
      QrFindCorNum.ParamByName('aLotType').Value := StrToInt(edLotID.Text);
      QrFindCorNum.Open;

      if Not QrfindCorNum.Eof then
      begin
        LotNum6.Text  := QrFindCorNum.fieldByName('Num6').AsString;
        LotNum2.Text  := QrFindCorNum.fieldByName('Num2').AsString;
        LotNum31.Text := QrFindCorNum.fieldByName('Num31').AsString;
        LotNum32.Text := QrFindCorNum.fieldByName('Num32').AsString;
        LotNum33.Text := QrFindCorNum.fieldByName('Num33').AsString;
        LotNum34.Text := QrFindCorNum.fieldByName('Num33').AsString;
      end
      else
      begin
        LotNum6.Text  := '';
        LotNum2.Text  := '';
        LotNum31.Text := '';
        LotNum32.Text := '';
        LotNum33.Text := '';
        LotNum34.Text := '';
      end
    finally
      QrFindCorNum.Free;
    end;
  end;
end;

function TfMain.foundCorNum:Boolean;
var QrFindCorNum : TABSQuery;
begin
  result := false;
  with Dm do
  begin
    try
      QrFindCorNum := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
      QrFindCorNum.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
      QrFindCorNum.Close;
      QrFindCorNum.SQL.Clear;
      QrFindCorNum.SQL.Add('Select * from CorrectNum');
      QrFindCorNum.SQL.Add('where (CrDate = :aCrDate) and (LotType = :aLotType)');

      QrFindCorNum.ParamByName('aCrDate').Value  := DateTostr(DatePick.Date);
      QrFindCorNum.ParamByName('aLotType').Value := StrToInt(edLotID.Text);
      QrFindCorNum.Open;

      if QrfindCorNum.Eof then
        result := false
      else
      begin
        result := true;
      end;
    finally
      QrFindCorNum.Free;
    end;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.EdNumberKeyPress(Sender: TObject; var Key: Char);
Var Num,LastStr,fileName: String;
begin
  Num := EdNumber.Text;
  //showmessage(Num);
  if Not (key in ['0'..'9','-','*','+','.','/', #13, #32, #8]) then
    Key := #0
  else
  if Key in [#13, #32 ] then
  begin
    //perform(Wm_NEXTDLGCTL,0,0);
    if ((length(Num)=1)and(Num[1] in ['*','.','/','-','+']))or(EdNumber.Text = '') then
    begin
      Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\fail.wav';
      if SoundOn then
        sndPlaySound(pchar(filename),SND_NODEFAULT Or SND_ASYNC);
      MessageDlg('กรุณาป้อนเลขให้ถูกต้อง', mtInformation, [mbOk], 0);
      edNumber.SetFocus;
      edNumber.SelectAll;
      exit;
    end
    else
      EdValue.SetFocus;

    key := #0;
  end;

  if Key in ['.'] then
  begin
    if (edNumber.SelLength = 0) then
    begin
      if (length(Num)=2) and (Num <> '19') then key := #0;
      if (length(Num)=1) and (Num[1] in ['0'..'9','/','+','-']) then key := #0;
      if (length(Num)>2)  then key := #0;
    end;
  end;

  if Key in ['-'] then
  begin
    if (edNumber.SelLength = 0) then
    begin
      if (length(Num)=1) and (Num[1] in ['/','+']) then key := #0;
      if (length(Num)>2) then key := #0;
      if (length(Num) > 0) then
      begin
        LastStr := copy(Num,length(Num),1);
        if LastStr[1] in ['-','+','/','.'] then Key := #0;
      end
    end;
  end;

  if Key in ['+'] then
  begin
    if (edNumber.SelLength = 0) then
    begin
      if (length(Num)<>2) then key := #0;
      if (length(Num)=2) and ((Not (Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])) or (Not (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']))) then key := #0;
    end;
  end;

  if Key in ['/'] then
  begin
    if (edNumber.SelLength = 0) then
    begin
      //Showmessage(string(Num[1]));
      if (length(Num) = 1) then
        if Num[1] in ['-','+','/','.'] then key := #0;
      if (length(Num)>1) then key := #0;
    end;
  end;

  if (length(Num) = 5) then
    if edNumber.SelLength = 0 then
      key := #0;

  if key in ['0'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\0.wav';
  if key in ['1'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\1.wav';
  if key in ['2'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\2.wav';
  if key in ['3'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\3.wav';
  if key in ['4'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\4.wav';
  if key in ['5'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\5.wav';
  if key in ['6'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\6.wav';
  if key in ['7'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\7.wav';
  if key in ['8'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\8.wav';
  if key in ['9'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\9.wav';
  if key in ['*'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\ok.wav';
  if key in ['/'] then
  begin
    if (length(edNumber.Text)=0)or(edNumber.SelLength = length(edNumber.Text)) then
      Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\lang.wav'
    else
      Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\nar.wav';
  end;

  if SoundOn then
    sndPlaySound(pchar(filename),SND_NODEFAULT Or SND_ASYNC);



end;

procedure TfMain.SumNGrid(NGrid,SGrid: TNiceGrid; StartRow: Integer);
Var SumCol: Array of Real;
    i,j: integer;
begin
  Setlength(SumCol,NGrid.ColCount-2);
  With NGrid do
  begin
    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
      SumCol[i-1] := 0;

    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
      for j := 0 To NGrid.RowCount-1 do
        SumCol[i-1] := SumCol[i-1] + txtToFloat(NGrid[i,j]);

    for i := 0 to ColCount-3 do
    begin
      //SumGrid[0,i+StartRow] := formatFloat('###,##0.##', SumCol[i]);
      SGrid[0,i+StartRow] := formatFloat('###,##0.##', SumCol[i]);
    end;
  end;
  Setlength(SumCol,1);
end;

procedure TfMain.EdNumberKeyDown(Sender: TObject; var Key: Word;
  Shift: TShiftState);
begin
  if Key = VK_Down then
  begin
    EdDwnNumber.SetFocus;
    Key := 0;
  end;

  if Key = VK_Escape then
  begin
    ChkEdit.Checked := false;
    EdValue.sStyle.Painting.Color := clWindow;
    EdValue.sStyle.Selection.Color:= clWindow;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.EdDwnNumberKeyDown(Sender: TObject; var Key: Word;
  Shift: TShiftState);
begin
  if Key = VK_Up then
  begin
    EdNumber.SetFocus;
    Key := 0;
  end;

  if Key = VK_Escape then
  begin
    ChkEdit.Checked := false;
    EdValue.sStyle.Painting.Color := clWindow;
    EdValue.sStyle.Selection.Color:= clWindow;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.EdDwnNumberKeyPress(Sender: TObject; var Key: Char);
Var Num,LastStr,filename: String;
begin
  Num := edDwnNumber.Text;
  if Not (key in ['0'..'9','-','*','.','/', #13, #32, #8]) then
    Key := #0
  else
  if Key in [#13, #32 ] then
  begin
    if ((length(Num)=1)and(Num[1] in ['*','.','/','-','+']))or(EdDwnNumber.Text = '') then
    begin
      Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\fail.wav';
      if SoundOn then
        sndPlaySound(pchar(filename),SND_NODEFAULT Or SND_ASYNC);    
      MessageDlg('กรุณาป้อนเลขให้ถูกต้อง', mtInformation, [mbOk], 0);
      edDwnNumber.SetFocus;
      edDwnNumber.SelectAll;
      exit;
    end
    else
      EdValue.SetFocus;
      
    key := #0;
  end;

    if Key in ['.'] then
  begin
    if (edDwnNumber.SelLength = 0) then
    begin
      if (length(Num)=2) and (Num <> '19') then key := #0;
      if (length(Num)=1) and (Num[1] in ['0'..'9','/','+','-']) then key := #0;
      if (length(Num)>2)  then key := #0;
    end;
  end;

  if Key in ['-'] then
  begin
    if (edDwnNumber.SelLength = 0) then
    begin
      if (length(Num)=1) and (Num[1] in ['/','+']) then key := #0;
      if (length(Num)>1) then key := #0;
      if (length(Num) > 0) then
      begin
        LastStr := copy(Num,length(Num),1);
        if LastStr[1] in ['-','+','/','.'] then Key := #0;
      end
    end;
  end;

  if Key in ['+'] then
  begin
    if (edDwnNumber.SelLength = 0) then
    begin
      if (length(Num)<>2) then key := #0;
      if (length(Num)=2) and ((Not (Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])) or (Not (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']))) then key := #0;
    end;
  end;

  if Key in ['/'] then
  begin
    if (edDwnNumber.SelLength = 0) then
    begin
      if (length(Num) = 1) then
        if Num[1] in ['-','+','/','.'] then key := #0;
      if (length(Num)>1) then key := #0;
    end;
  end;

  if (length(Num) = 5) then
    if edDwnNumber.SelLength = 0 then
      key := #0;

  if key in ['0'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\0.wav';
  if key in ['1'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\1.wav';
  if key in ['2'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\2.wav';
  if key in ['3'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\3.wav';
  if key in ['4'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\4.wav';
  if key in ['5'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\5.wav';
  if key in ['6'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\6.wav';
  if key in ['7'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\7.wav';
  if key in ['8'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\8.wav';
  if key in ['9'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\9.wav';
  if key in ['*'] then
    Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\ok.wav';
  if key in ['/'] then
  begin
    if (length(edDwnNumber.Text)=0)or(edDwnNumber.SelLength = length(edDwnNumber.Text)) then
      Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\lang.wav'
    else
      Filename := ExtractFilePath(Application.ExeName) + 'Sound\nar.wav';
  end;

  if SoundOn then
    sndPlaySound(pchar(filename),SND_NODEFAULT Or SND_ASYNC);

end;

  IniFile := TIniFile.Create(ChangeFileExt(Application.ExeName,'.ini'));

  PageControlCutNum.TabIndex := IniFile.ReadInteger('TAB','TabCut',3);
  MainPageControl.TabIndex   := IniFile.ReadInteger('TAB','TabMain',0);
  IniFile.Free;
  SoundOn := true;

  Statusbar.Panels[0].Text := 'Big LOTTO 2.0 (ติดต่อ โทร : 084-0643183) - '+Dm.Database.DatabaseFileName;
  Windowstate := wsMaximized;
  isup :=  true;
end;

procedure TfMain.QrSortNum(Num,Value:String; IsUpNum:Boolean);
Var Nums: Array of String[3];
    Price: Array[1..6] of real;
    NumCount,i,StarPos: Integer;
    Price1,Price2: String;
begin
  StarPos:= Pos('*',Value);
  if (foundChar(Value,'*')) then
  begin
    StarPos:= Pos('*',Value);
    if StarPos = 1 then
    begin
      Price1 := Copy(Value,2,length(Value)-1);
      Price2 := Copy(Value,2,length(Value)-1);
    end
    else
    begin
      Price1 := Copy(Value,1,StarPos-1);
      Price2 := Copy(Value,StarPos+1,length(Value)-StarPos);
    end;
  end;
  
  with Numslist do
  begin
      if (Num[1] = Num[2]) and (Num[2] = Num[3]) and (Num[3] = Num[1]) then
      begin
        setlength(Nums,1);
        Nums[0] := Num;

         if IsUpNum then
         begin
          ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
         end
         else
         begin
          ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
         end;

        NumCount := 1;
      end
      else
      if (Num[1] <> Num[2]) and (Num[2] <> Num[3]) and (Num[3] <> Num[1]) then
      begin
         setlength(Nums,6);
         Nums[0] := Num;
         Nums[1] := Num[1]+Num[3]+Num[2];
         Nums[2] := Num[2]+Num[1]+Num[3];
         Nums[3] := Num[2]+Num[3]+Num[1];
         Nums[4] := Num[3]+Num[1]+Num[2];
         Nums[5] := Num[3]+Num[2]+Num[1];

         if IsUpNum then
         begin
          ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 5 do
          begin
            ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end
         else
         begin
          ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 5 do
          begin
            ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end;

         NumCount := 6;
      end
      else
      if (Num[1] = Num[2])and(Num[2] <> Num[3]) then
      begin
         setlength(Nums,3);
         Nums[0] := Num;
         Nums[1] := Num[1]+Num[3]+Num[1];
         Nums[2] := Num[3]+Num[1]+Num[1];

         if IsUpNum then
         begin
          ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 2 do
          begin
            ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end
         else
         begin
          ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 2 do
          begin
            ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end;


         NumCount := 3;
      end
      else
      if (Num[2] = Num[3])and(Num[3] <> Num[1]) then
      begin
         setlength(Nums,3);
         Nums[0] := Num;
         Nums[1] := Num[2]+Num[1]+Num[2];
         Nums[2] := Num[2]+Num[2]+Num[1];

         if IsUpNum then
         begin
          ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 2 do
          begin
            ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end
         else
         begin
          ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 2 do
          begin
            ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end;

         NumCount := 3;
      end
      else
      if (Num[1] = Num[3])and(Num[3] <> Num[2]) then
      begin
         setlength(Nums,3);
         Nums[0] := Num;
         Nums[1] := Num[1]+Num[1]+Num[2];
         Nums[2] := Num[2]+Num[1]+Num[1];

         if IsUpNum then
         begin
          ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 2 do
          begin
            ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end
         else
         begin
          ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 2 do
          begin
            ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end;

         NumCount := 3;
      end;
   end;
end;

procedure TfMain.SortNum1(Num,Value:String; IsUpNum:Boolean);
Var Nums: Array of String[3];
    Price: Array[1..6] of real;
    NumCount,i,StarPos: Integer;
    Price1,Price2: String;
begin
  StarPos:= Pos('*',Value);
  if (foundChar(Value,'*')) then
  begin
    StarPos:= Pos('*',Value);
    if StarPos = 1 then
    begin
      Price1 := Copy(Value,2,length(Value)-1);
      Price2 := Copy(Value,2,length(Value)-1);
    end
    else
    if (StarPos > 1)and(StarPos < length(Value)) then
    begin
      Price1 := Copy(Value,1,StarPos-1);
      Price2 := Copy(Value,StarPos+1,length(Value)-StarPos);
    end
    else
    if (StarPos = length(Value)) then
    begin
      Price1 := Copy(Value,1,StarPos-1);
      Price2 := Price1;
    end;
  end;
  
  with Numslist do
  begin
      if (Num[1] = Num[2]) and (Num[2] = Num[3]) and (Num[3] = Num[1]) then
      begin
        setlength(Nums,1);
        Nums[0] := Num;

         if IsUpNum then
         begin
          Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
         end
         else
         begin
          Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          Array3[3,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[3,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
         end;

        NumCount := 1;
      end
      else
      if (Num[1] <> Num[2]) and (Num[2] <> Num[3]) and (Num[3] <> Num[1]) then
      begin
         setlength(Nums,6);
         Nums[0] := Num;
         Nums[1] := Num[1]+Num[3]+Num[2];
         Nums[2] := Num[2]+Num[1]+Num[3];
         Nums[3] := Num[2]+Num[3]+Num[1];
         Nums[4] := Num[3]+Num[1]+Num[2];
         Nums[5] := Num[3]+Num[2]+Num[1];

         if IsUpNum then
         begin
          Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 5 do
          begin
            Array3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            Array3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end
         else
         begin
          Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          Array3[3,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[3,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 5 do
          begin
            Array3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            Array3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end;

         NumCount := 6;
      end
      else
      if (Num[1] = Num[2])and(Num[2] <> Num[3]) then
      begin
         setlength(Nums,3);
         Nums[0] := Num;
         Nums[1] := Num[1]+Num[3]+Num[1];
         Nums[2] := Num[3]+Num[1]+Num[1];

         if IsUpNum then
         begin
          Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 2 do
          begin
            Array3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            Array3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end
         else
         begin
          Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          Array3[3,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[3,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 2 do
          begin
            Array3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            Array3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end;


         NumCount := 3;
      end
      else
      if (Num[2] = Num[3])and(Num[3] <> Num[1]) then
      begin
         setlength(Nums,3);
         Nums[0] := Num;
         Nums[1] := Num[2]+Num[1]+Num[2];
         Nums[2] := Num[2]+Num[2]+Num[1];

         if IsUpNum then
         begin
          Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 2 do
          begin
            Array3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            Array3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end
         else
         begin
          Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          Array3[3,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[3,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 2 do
          begin
            Array3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            Array3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end;

         NumCount := 3;
      end
      else
      if (Num[1] = Num[3])and(Num[3] <> Num[2]) then
      begin
         setlength(Nums,3);
         Nums[0] := Num;
         Nums[1] := Num[1]+Num[1]+Num[2];
         Nums[2] := Num[2]+Num[1]+Num[1];

         if IsUpNum then
         begin
          Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 2 do
          begin
            Array3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            Array3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end
         else
         begin
          Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          Array3[3,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[3,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
          for i := 1 to 2 do
          begin
            Array3[0,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := Nums[i];
            Array3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(Array3[3,StrToInt(Nums[i])],0) + StrToFloat(Price2) );
          end;
         end;

         NumCount := 3;
      end;
   end;
end;

procedure TfMain.SortNumCut(Num,Value:String; IsUpNum:Boolean);
Var Nums: Array of String[3];
    Price: Array[1..6] of real;
    NumCount,i,StarPos: Integer;
    Price1,Price2: String;
begin
  StarPos:= Pos('*',Value);
  if (foundChar(Value,'*')) then
  begin
    StarPos:= Pos('*',Value);
    if StarPos = 1 then
    begin
      Price1 := Copy(Value,2,length(Value)-1);
      Price2 := Copy(Value,2,length(Value)-1);
    end
    else
    if (StarPos > 1)and(StarPos < length(Value)) then
    begin
      Price1 := Copy(Value,1,StarPos-1);
      Price2 := Copy(Value,StarPos+1,length(Value)-StarPos);
    end
    else
    if (StarPos = length(Value)) then
    begin
      Price1 := Copy(Value,1,StarPos-1);
      Price2 := Price1;
    end;
  end;

  with Numslist do
  begin
      if (Num[1] = Num[2]) and (Num[2] = Num[3]) and (Num[3] = Num[1]) then
      begin
        setlength(Nums,1);
        Nums[0] := Num;

         if IsUpNum then
         begin
          ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
         end
         else

          ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] := Num;
          ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Num)] := FormatFloat('##0.##', StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Num)],0) +StrToFloat(Price1) );
         end;

        NumCount := 1;
      end
      else
      if (Num[1] <> Num[2]) and (Num[2] <> Num[3]) and (Num[3] <> Num[1]) then
      begin
         setlength(Nums,6);
         Nums[0] := Num;
         Nums[1] := Num[1]+Num[3]+Num[2];
         Nums[2] := Num[2]+Num[1]+Num[3];
         Nums[3] := Num[2]+Num[3]+Num[1];

         Nums[5] := Num[3]+Num[2]+Num[1];

procedure TfMain.SortNum2(Num,Value:String);
Var Nums: Array of String[3];
    Price: Array[1..6] of real;
    NumCount,i,StarPos: Integer;
    Price1,Price2: String;
begin
  StarPos:= Pos('*',Value);
  if (foundChar(Value,'*')) then
  begin
    StarPos:= Pos('*',Value);
    if StarPos = 1 then
    begin
      Price1 := Copy(Value,2,length(Value)-1);
      Price2 := Copy(Value,2,length(Value)-1);
    end
    else
    if (starPos > 1)and (StarPos < length(Value)) then
    begin
      Price1 := Copy(Value,1,StarPos-1);
      Price2 := Copy(Value,StarPos+1,length(Value)-StarPos);
    end
    else
    if (StarPos = length(Value)) then
    begin
      Price1 := Copy(Value,1,StarPos-1);
      Price2 := Price1
    end;
  end;
  
  with Numslist do
  begin
      if (Num[1] = Num[2]) and (Num[2] = Num[3]) and (Num[3] = Num[1]) then
      begin
        setlength(Nums,1);
        Nums[0] := Num;
        NumCount := 1;
      end
      else
      if (Num[1] <> Num[2]) and (Num[2] <> Num[3]) and (Num[3] <> Num[1]) then
      begin
         setlength(Nums,6);
         Nums[0] := Num;
         Nums[1] := Num[1]+Num[3]+Num[2];
         Nums[2] := Num[2]+Num[1]+Num[3];
         Nums[3] := Num[2]+Num[3]+Num[1];
         Nums[4] := Num[3]+Num[1]+Num[2];
         Nums[5] := Num[3]+Num[2]+Num[1];
         NumCount := 6;
      end
      else
      if (Num[1] = Num[2])and(Num[2] <> Num[3]) then
      begin
         setlength(Nums,3);
         Nums[0] := Num;
         Nums[1] := Num[1]+Num[3]+Num[1];
         Nums[2] := Num[3]+Num[1]+Num[1];
         NumCount := 3;
      end
      else
      if (Num[2] = Num[3])and(Num[3] <> Num[1]) then
      begin
         setlength(Nums,3);
         Nums[0] := Num;
         Nums[1] := Num[2]+Num[1]+Num[2];
         Nums[2] := Num[2]+Num[2]+Num[1];
         NumCount := 3;
      end
      else
      if (Num[1] = Num[3])and(Num[3] <> Num[2]) then
      begin
         setlength(Nums,3);
         Nums[0] := Num;
         Nums[1] := Num[1]+Num[1]+Num[2];
         Nums[2] := Num[2]+Num[1]+Num[1];
         NumCount := 3;
      end;
   
      Items.Clear;
      Items.BeginUpdate;
      for i := 0 to NumCount-1 do
      begin
        with Items.Add do
        begin
          Caption := Nums[i];
          if i = 0 then
            SubItems.Add(Price1)
          else
            SubItems.Add(Price2);
        end;
      end;
      Items.EndUpdate;
   
   end;
end;

procedure TfMain.SortNum(Str,Value:String);
Var i,j,k,l,Count,StarPos: integer;
    Nums: Array[1..6] of String[5];
    temp: Char;
    Num,Price1,Price2: String;
    found: Boolean;
begin
   Num := trim(Str);
   Count := length(Num);
      l := 1;
      for i := 1 To Count do
      begin
        Temp   := Num[1];
        Num[1] := Num[2];
        Num[2] := Num[3];
        Num[3] := Temp;
        for j := 1 to Count-1 do
        begin
          Temp   := Num[2];
          Num[2] := Num[3];
          Num[3] := temp;
          for k := 1 to Count-2 do
          begin
            temp   := Num[3];
            Num[3] := temp;
              Nums[l] := Num[1]+Num[2]+Num[3]; //Array
            inc(l);
          end;
        end;
      end;

      With NumsList do
      begin
        Clear;
        if (foundChar(Value,'*')) then
        begin
          StarPos:= Pos('*',Value);
          if StarPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(Value,2,length(Value)-1);
            Price2 := Copy(Value,2,length(Value)-1);
          end
          else
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(Value,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(Value,StarPos+1,length(Value)-StarPos);
          end;
        end;

        Items.BeginUpdate;
        for i := 1 to l-1 do
        begin
          found := false;
          for j := 0 to Items.Count-1 do
          begin
            found := Nums[i] = Items[j].Caption;
            if found then break;
          end;

          if Not found then
          begin
            with Items.Add do
            begin
              Caption := Nums[i];

              if Nums[i] = Num then
                SubItems.Add(Price1)
              else
                SubItems.Add(Price2);
            end;
          end;
        end;
        Items.EndUpdate;
        //NumsList.CustomSort(@ByFirst, 0);
    end;
end;

procedure TfMain.Sort4Num(Str,Value:String);
Var i,j,k,l,Count,StarPos: integer;
    Nums: Array[1..24] of String[5];
    temp: Char;
    Num,Price1,Price2: String;
    found: Boolean;
begin
   Num := trim(Str);
   Count := length(Num);
      l := 1;
      for i := 1 To Count do
      begin
        Temp   := Num[1];
        Num[1] := Num[2];
        Num[2] := Num[3];
        Num[3] := Num[4];
        Num[4] := Temp;
        for j := 1 to 3 do
        begin
          Temp   := Num[2];
          Num[2] := Num[3];
          Num[3] := Num[4];
          Num[4] := temp;
          for k := 1 to 2 do
          begin
            temp   := Num[3];
            Num[3] := Num[4];
            Num[4] := temp;
            //if Rdb41.Checked then
              //Nums[l] := Num[1]+Num[2]+Num[3]+Num[4] //Array
            //else
              Nums[l] := Num[2]+Num[3]+Num[4]; //Array
            inc(l);
          end;
        end;
      end;


      With NumsList do
      begin
        Clear;
        if (foundChar(Value,'*')) then
        begin
          StarPos:= Pos('*',Value);
          if StarPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(Value,2,length(Value)-1);
            Price2 := Copy(Value,2,length(Value)-1);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos > 1) and (StarPos < length(Value)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(Value,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(Value,StarPos+1,length(Value)-StarPos);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos = length(Value)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(Value,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Price1;
          end;
        end;

        Items.BeginUpdate;
        for i := 1 to l-1 do
        begin
          found := false;
          for j := 0 to Items.Count-1 do
          begin
            found := Nums[i] = Items[j].Caption;
            if found then break;
          end;

          if Not found then
          begin
            with Items.Add do
            begin
              Caption := Nums[i];

              if Nums[i] = Num then
                SubItems.Add(Price1)
              else
                SubItems.Add(Price2);
            end;
          end;
        end;
        Items.EndUpdate;
    end;
end;


procedure TfMain.InputGridKeyDown(Sender: TObject; var Key: Word;
  Shift: TShiftState);
Var QrDelete: TABSQuery;
    i: Integer;
begin
  With InPutGrid do
  if Key = VK_Delete then
  begin
    with Dm do
    begin
      QrDelete := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
      QrDelete.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;

      if (SelectArea.Bottom - (SelectArea.Top-1)) > 1 then
      begin
        if MessageDlg('ต้องลบรายการที่ป้อนจำนวน  "'+IntToStr((SelectArea.Bottom - (SelectArea.Top-1)))+'"  รายการ ใช่หรือไม่?',
          mtConfirmation, [mbYes, mbNo], 0) = mrYes then
        begin
          progressbar2.MinValue := 0;
          Progressbar2.MaxValue := (SelectArea.Bottom - SelectArea.Top)+1;
          Progressbar2.Progress := 0;
          Pbar.Visible := true;

          for i := SelectArea.Bottom Downto SelectArea.Top  do
          begin
            QrDelete.Close;
            QrDelete.SQL.Clear;
            QrDelete.SQL.Add('Delete from Data');
            QrDelete.SQL.Add('Where ID = '+InputGrid[3,i]);
            QrDelete.ExecSQL;

            DelTranslate(InputGrid[0,i],InputGrid[1,i],InputGrid[2,i],InputGrid[3,i]);
            if InputGrid.RowCount = 1 then
              InputGrid.Clear;
            InputGrid.DeleteRow(i);
            Progressbar2.Progress := Progressbar2.Progress + 1;
          end;
          Progressbar2.Progress := 0 ;
          Pbar.Visible := false;
        end;
      end
      else
      begin
          for i := SelectArea.Bottom Downto SelectArea.Top  do
          begin
            QrDelete.Close;
            QrDelete.SQL.Clear;
            QrDelete.SQL.Add('Delete from Data');
            QrDelete.SQL.Add('Where ID = '+InputGrid[3,i]);
            QrDelete.ExecSQL;

            DelTranslate(InputGrid[0,i],InputGrid[1,i],InputGrid[2,i],InputGrid[3,i]);
            if InputGrid.RowCount = 1 then
              InputGrid.Clear;
            InputGrid.DeleteRow(i);
          end;
      end;
      QrDelete.Free;

      lbItems1.Caption := IntToStr(InputGrid.RowCount)+'  รายการ';
      SumNGrid(NiceGrid1,SumGrid,0);
      SumNGrid(NiceGrid2,SumGrid,7);
      SumNGrid(NiceGrid3,SumGrid,13);
      SumsGrid(SumGrid);
    end;
  end;


  if Key = VK_F2 then
  begin
    with InputGrid do
    begin
      if InputGrid[1,Row] <> '' then
      begin
        ChkEdit.Checked := Not ChkLook.Checked;
        edNumber.Text := InputGrid[0,Row];
        edDwnNumber.Text := '';
        edValue.Text  := InputGrid[1,Row];
        EdNumber.SetFocus;
      end
      else
      begin
        ChkEdit.Checked := Not ChkLook.Checked;
        edDwnNumber.Text := InputGrid[0,Row];
        edNumber.Text := '';
        edValue.Text  := InputGrid[2,Row];
        EdDwnNumber.SetFocus;
      end;
    end;
    Key := 0;
  end;
  
end;

procedure TfMain.Timer1Timer(Sender: TObject);
begin
  lbClock.Caption := formatDateTime('HH:MM:SS',Now);
end;

procedure TfMain.Translate1(Num,ValUp,ValDwn,ID: String);
Var i,j,k,StarPos,LobPos: integer;
    Price1,Price2,sNum : String;
    found: boolean;
begin

  with Dm do
  begin
    with Numslist,InputGrid do
    begin
      Price1 := '0';
      Price2 := '0';

      if ValUp <> '' then
      begin
        StarPos:= Pos('*',ValUP);
        LobPos := Pos('-',ValUP);

        if (Not (foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not (foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
        begin
            Price1 := ValUp;
            Price2 := '0';
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then
        begin
          if StarPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos > 1)and(StarPos < length(ValUp)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,StarPos+1,length(ValUp)-StarPos);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos = length(ValUp)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Price1;
          end;
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
        begin
          if LobPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
            Price2 := '0';
          end
          else
          if (LobPos > 1)and(LobPos < length(ValUp)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,LobPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,LobPos+1,length(ValUp)-LobPos);
          end
          else
          if (LobPos = length(ValUp)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,LobPos-1);
            Price2 := '0';
          end;
        end;


 //---------------------------- เลข 1 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid1 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- วิ่งบน ----------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(length(Num)=1) and(StarPos =0)and(LobPos = 0) then //    and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array1[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array1[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array1[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array1[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหน้าบน ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in ['-'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[1])] = '' then
              Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[1])] :=  Num[1];
            Array1[2,StrToInt(Num[1])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array1[2,StrToInt(Num[1])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักกลางบน ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['-'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['-'])and((length(Num)=3))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] = '' then
              Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  Num[2];
            Array1[3,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array1[3,StrToInt(Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหลังบน ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['-'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in  ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] = '' then
              Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  Num[2];
            Array1[4,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array1[4,StrToInt(Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//
   
        end;
   
 //---------------------------- เลข 2 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid2 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;

          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน เลชคู่ -----------------//
          if (length(Num)=1)and(Num[1] in ['.']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน เลขพี่น้อง -----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['.'])and(Num[2] in ['.']) then
          begin

            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              if i = 9 then
                Num := IntToStr(i)+'0'
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i+1);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
                
              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

            for i := 9 Downto 0 do
            begin
              if i = 0 then
                Num := IntToStr(i)+'9'
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i-1);

              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;

              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน รูดหน้า-----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['/']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := String(Num[1])+IntToStr(i);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน รูดหลัง-----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['/'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(Num[2]);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน 19 ประตู----------------//
          if (length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['1'])and(Num[2] in ['9'])and(Num[3] in ['.'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            sNum := Num;
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := String(sNum[4])+IntToStr(i);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(sNum[4]);
              if intToStr(i) <> String(sNum[4]) then
              begin
                if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                  Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
                Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
              end;
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------2 ตัวโต๊ด --------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Length(Num)=2) and (LobPos = 1)  then
          begin
            if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array2[2,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[2,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------2 ตัวตรง - โต๊ด --------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Length(Num)=2) and (LobPos > 1)  then
          begin
            if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            Array2[2,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[2,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price2));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวคูณบน--------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and ((foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
           for i := 1 to 2 do
           begin
            if i = 1 then
            begin
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end
            else
            begin
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] :=  Num[2]+Num[1];
              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])],0)+StrToFloat(Price2));
            end;
           end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวมี ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['+']) and (length(Num)=3) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] = '' then
              Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] :=  Num[1]+Num[2];
            Array2[3,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[3,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวหน้า -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['-']) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] = '' then
              Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] :=  Num[1]+Num[2];
            Array2[4,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[4,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวหลัง -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['-']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9']) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
           if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[3])] = '' then
              Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[3])] :=  Num[2]+Num[3];
            Array2[5,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[3])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[5,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[3])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//


        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 3 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid3 do
        begin
  
          //---------------------------3 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------3 ตัวโต๊ด --------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))and(LobPos = 1)  then
          begin
            if Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array3[2,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array3[2,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- 3 ตัวตรง-โต๊ด -----------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))and(LobPos > 1)  then
          begin
            if Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            Array3[2,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array3[2,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price2));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //-------------------------- 3 ตัวบนคูณ -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then
          begin
                SortNum1(Num,ValUp,True);
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //-------------------------- 4 ตัวบนคูณเป็น 3 ตัว -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and (Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then
          begin
                Sort4Num(Num,ValUp);

                for j := Items.Count-1 Downto 0 do
                begin
                  if Array3[0,StrToInt(Items[j].Caption)] = '' then
                    Array3[0,StrToInt(Items[j].Caption)] := Items[j].Caption;
                  Array3[1,StrToInt(Items[j].Caption)] := FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Items[j].Caption)],0)+StrToFloat(Items[j].SubItems[0]));
                end;
              
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//


        end;


 //---------------------------- เลข 4 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid4 do
        begin
          //---------------------------4 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            found := false;
            if length(Array4) > 0 then
            for i := 0 to length(Array4)-1 do
            begin
              if  Array4[i,0] = Num then
              begin
                found := true;
                break;
              end;
            end;
            if Not found then
            begin
              setlength(Array4,length(Array4)+1,3);
              Array4[length(Array4)-1,0] := Num;
              Array4[length(Array4)-1,1] := Price1;
            end
            else
            begin
              Array4[i,1] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array4[i,1],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------4 ตัวโต๊ด ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(LobPos = 1) then // and(foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
          begin
            found := false;
            for i := 0 to length(Array4)-1 do
            begin
              if  Array4[i,0] = Num then
              begin
                found := true;
                break;
              end;
            end;
            if Not found then
            begin
              setlength(Array4,length(Array4)+1,3);
              Array4[length(Array4)-1,0] := Num;
              Array4[length(Array4)-1,2] := Price1;
            end
            else
            begin
              Array4[i,2] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array4[i,2],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------4 ตัวตรง - โต๊ด --------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(LobPos > 1) then // and(foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
          begin
            found := false;
            for i := 0 to length(Array4)-1 do
            begin
              if  Array4[i,0] = Num then
              begin
                found := true;
                Array4[i,1] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array4[i,1],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
                Array4[i,2] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array4[i,2],0)+StrToFloat(Price2));

                break;
              end;
            end;
            if Not found then
            begin
              setlength(Array4,length(Array4)+1,3);
              Array4[length(Array4)-1,0] := Num;
              Array4[length(Array4)-1,1] := Price1;
              Array4[length(Array4)-1,2] := Price2;
            end
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 5 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid5 do
        begin
          //---------------------------5 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            found := false;
            for i := 0 to length(Array5)-1 do
            begin
              if  Array5[i,0] = Num then
              begin
                found := true;
                break;
              end;
            end;
            if Not found then
            begin
              setlength(Array5,length(Array5)+1,3);
              Array5[length(Array5)-1,0] := Num;
              Array5[length(Array5)-1,1] := Price1;
            end
            else
            begin
              Array5[i,1] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array5[i,1],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------5 ตัวโต๊ด ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(LobPos = 1) then //and(foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
          begin
            found := false;
            for i := 0 to length(Array5)-1 do
            begin
              if  Array5[i,0] = Num then
              begin
                found := true;
                break;
              end;
            end;
            if Not found then
            begin
              setlength(Array5,length(Array5)+1,3);
              Array5[length(Array5)-1,0] := Num;
              Array5[length(Array5)-1,2] := Price1;
            end
            else
            begin
              Array5[i,2] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array5[i,2],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//


          //---------------------------5 ตัวตรง - โต๊ด --------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(LobPos > 1) then // and(foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
          begin
            found := false;
            for i := 0 to length(Array5)-1 do
            begin
              if  Array5[i,0] = Num then
              begin
                found := true;
                Array5[i,1] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array5[i,1],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
                Array5[i,2] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array5[i,2],0)+StrToFloat(Price2));

                break;
              end;
            end;
            if Not found then
            begin
              setlength(Array5,length(Array5)+1,3);
              Array5[length(Array5)-1,0] := Num;
              Array5[length(Array5)-1,1] := Price1;
              Array5[length(Array5)-1,2] := Price2;
            end
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//


       {
          //---------------------------5 ตัวตรง - โต๊ด --------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(LobPos > 1) then // and(foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
          begin
            found := false;
            for i := 0 to length(Array5)-1 do
            begin
              if  Array5[i,0] = Num then
              begin
                found := true;
                break;
              end;
            end;
            if Not found then
            begin
              setlength(Array5,length(Array5)+1,3);
              Array5[length(Array5)-1,0] := Num;
              Array5[length(Array5)-1,1] := Price1;
              Array5[length(Array5)-1,2] := Price2;
            end
            else
            begin
              Array5[i,1] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array5[i,1],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
              Array5[i,2] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array5[i,2],0)+StrToFloat(Price2));
            end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//
         }
        end; 
      end
      else   //---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------//
      begin

        Price1 := '0';
        Price2 := '0';
        StarPos:= Pos('*',ValDwn);
        LobPos := Pos('-',ValDwn);


        if (Not (foundChar(ValDwn,'*'))) and (Not (foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
        begin
            Price1 := ValDwn;
            Price2 := '0';
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValDwn,'*')) then
        begin
          if StarPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos > 1)and(StarPos < length(ValDwn)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,StarPos+1,length(ValDwn)-StarPos);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos = length(ValDwn)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Price1;
          end;
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValDwn,'-')) then
        begin
          if LobPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
            Price2 := '0';
          end
          else
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,1,LobPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,LobPos+1,length(ValDwn)-LobPos);
          end;
        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 1 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid1 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- วิ่งล่าง ----------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=1) and (StarPos =0) and (LobPos = 0) then //and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array1[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array1[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array1[5,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array1[5,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหน้าล่าง ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in ['-'])and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[1])] = '' then
              Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[1])] :=  Num[1];
            Array1[6,StrToInt(Num[1])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array1[6,StrToInt(Num[1])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหลังล่าง -------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['-'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in  ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] = '' then
              Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  Num[2];
            Array1[7,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array1[7,StrToInt(Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

        end;


 //---------------------------- เลข 2 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid2 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวล่าง ----------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and (Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวคูณล่าง-----------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and ((foundChar(ValDwn,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
           for i := 1 to 2 do
           begin
            if i = 1 then
            begin
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end
            else
            begin
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] :=  Num[2]+Num[1];
              Array2[6,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])],0)+StrToFloat(Price2));
            end;
           end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง เลชคู่ -----------------//
          if (length(Num)=1)and(Num[1] in ['.']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง เลขพี่น้อง -----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['.'])and(Num[2] in ['.']) then
          begin

            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              if i = 9 then
                Num := IntToStr(i)+'0'
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i+1);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
                
              Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

            for i := 9 Downto 0 do
            begin
              if i = 0 then
                Num := IntToStr(i)+'9'
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i-1);

              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;

              Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง รูดหน้า-----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['/']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := String(Num[1])+IntToStr(i);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง รูดหลัง-----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['/'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(Num[2]);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง 19 ประตู----------------//
          if (length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['1'])and(Num[2] in ['9'])and(Num[3] in ['.'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            sNum := Num;
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := String(sNum[4])+IntToStr(i);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(sNum[4]);
              if intToStr(i) <> String(sNum[4]) then
              begin
                if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                  Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
                Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
              end;
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------// 

        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 3 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid3 do
        begin
          //------------------------- 3 ตัวล่าง 1 ตัว ---------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array3[3,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array3[3,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //-------------------------- 3 ตัวล่างคูณ -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(length(Num)=3)and(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')) then
          begin
                SortNum1(Num,ValDwn,false);
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//
    

        end;
      end;  
    end;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.TranslateCut(Num,ValUp,ValDwn,ID: String);
Var i,j,k,StarPos,LobPos: integer;
    Price1,Price2,sNum : String;
    found: boolean;
begin

  with Dm do
  begin
    with Numslist,InputGrid do
    begin
      Price1 := '0';
      Price2 := '0';

      if ValUp <> '' then
      begin
        StarPos:= Pos('*',ValUP);
        LobPos := Pos('-',ValUP);

        if (Not (foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not (foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
        begin
            Price1 := ValUp;
            Price2 := '0';
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then
        begin
          if StarPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos > 1)and(StarPos < length(ValUp)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,StarPos+1,length(ValUp)-StarPos);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos = length(ValUp)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Price1;
          end;
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
        begin
          if LobPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
            Price2 := '0';
          end
          else
          if (LobPos >1)and(lobPos < length(ValUp)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,LobPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,LobPos+1,length(ValUp)-LobPos);
          end
          else
          if (lobPos = length(ValUp)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,LobPos-1);
            Price2 := '0';
          end;
        end;


 //---------------------------- เลข 1 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid1 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- วิ่งบน ----------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(length(Num)=1) and(StarPos =0)and(LobPos = 0) then //    and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if ArCut1[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              ArCut1[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            ArCut1[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหน้าบน ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in ['-'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if ArCut1[0,StrToInt(Num[1])] = '' then
              ArCut1[0,StrToInt(Num[1])] :=  Num[1];
            ArCut1[2,StrToInt(Num[1])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[2,StrToInt(Num[1])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักกลางบน ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['-'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['-'])and((length(Num)=3))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if ArCut1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] = '' then
              ArCut1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  Num[2];
            ArCut1[3,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[3,StrToInt(Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหลังบน ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['-'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in  ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if ArCut1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] = '' then
              ArCut1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  Num[2];
            ArCut1[4,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[4,StrToInt(Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//
   
        end;
   
 //---------------------------- เลข 2 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid2 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;

          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน เลชคู่ -----------------//
          if (length(Num)=1)and(Num[1] in ['.']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i);
              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน เลขพี่น้อง -----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['.'])and(Num[2] in ['.']) then
          begin

            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              if i = 9 then
                Num := IntToStr(i)+'0'
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i+1);
              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
                
              ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

            for i := 9 Downto 0 do
            begin
              if i = 0 then
                Num := IntToStr(i)+'9'
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i-1);

              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;

              ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน รูดหน้า-----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['/']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := String(Num[1])+IntToStr(i);
              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน รูดหลัง-----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['/'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(Num[2]);
              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน 19 ประตู----------------//
          if (length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['1'])and(Num[2] in ['9'])and(Num[3] in ['.'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            sNum := Num;
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := String(sNum[4])+IntToStr(i);
              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(sNum[4]);
              if intToStr(i) <> String(sNum[4]) then
              begin
                if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                  ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
                ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
              end;
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------2 ตัวโต๊ด --------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Length(Num)=2) and (LobPos = 1)  then
          begin
            if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            ArCut2[2,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[2,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวคูณบน--------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and ((foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
           for i := 1 to 2 do
           begin
            if i = 1 then
            begin
              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
