              ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end
            else
            begin
              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] :=  Num[2]+Num[1];
              ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[1,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])],0)+StrToFloat(Price2));
            end;
           end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวมี ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['+']) and (length(Num)=3) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] = '' then
              ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] :=  Num[1]+Num[2];
            ArCut2[3,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[3,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวหน้า -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['-']) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] = '' then
              ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] :=  Num[1]+Num[2];
            ArCut2[4,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[4,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวหลัง -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['-']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9']) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
           if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[3])] = '' then
              ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[3])] :=  Num[2]+Num[3];
            ArCut2[5,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[3])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[5,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[3])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//


        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 3 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid3 do
        begin
  
          //---------------------------3 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------3 ตัวโต๊ด --------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))and(LobPos = 1)  then
          begin
            if ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            ArCut3[2,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[2,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- 3 ตัวตรง-โต๊ด -----------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))and(LobPos <> 1)  then
          begin
            if ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            ArCut3[2,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[2,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price2));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //-------------------------- 3 ตัวบนคูณ -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then
          begin
                SortNumCut(Num,ValUp,True);
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //-------------------------- 4 ตัวบนคูณเป็น 3 ตัว -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and (Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then
          begin
                Sort4Num(Num,ValUp);

                for j := Items.Count-1 Downto 0 do
                begin
                  if ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Items[j].Caption)] = '' then
                    ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Items[j].Caption)] := Items[j].Caption;
                  ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Items[j].Caption)] := FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[1,StrToInt(Items[j].Caption)],0)+StrToFloat(Items[j].SubItems[0]));
                end;
              
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//


        end;


 //---------------------------- เลข 4 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid4 do
        begin
          //---------------------------4 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            found := false;
            if length(ArCut4) > 0 then
            for i := 0 to length(ArCut4)-1 do
            begin
              if  ArCut4[i,0] = Num then
              begin
                found := true;
                break;
              end;
            end;
            if Not found then
            begin
              setlength(ArCut4,length(ArCut4)+1,3);
              ArCut4[length(ArCut4)-1,0] := Num;
              ArCut4[length(ArCut4)-1,1] := Price1;
            end
            else
            begin
              ArCut4[i,1] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut4[i,1],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------4 ตัวโต๊ด ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(LobPos = 1) then // (foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
          begin
            found := false;
            for i := 0 to length(ArCut4)-1 do
            begin
              if  ArCut4[i,0] = Num then
              begin
                found := true;
                ArCut4[i,2] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut4[i,2],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
                break;
              end;
            end;
            if Not found then
            begin
              setlength(ArCut4,length(ArCut4)+1,3);
              ArCut4[length(ArCut4)-1,0] := Num;
              ArCut4[length(ArCut4)-1,2] := Price1;
            end
            //else
            //begin

            //end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------4 ตัวตรง - โต๊ด --------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(LobPos > 1) then // and(foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
          begin
            found := false;
            for i := 0 to length(ArCut4)-1 do
            begin
              if  ArCut4[i,0] = Num then
              begin
                found := true;
                ArCut4[i,1] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut4[i,1],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
                ArCut4[i,2] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut4[i,2],0)+StrToFloat(Price2));

                break;
              end;
            end;
            if Not found then
            begin
              setlength(ArCut4,length(ArCut4)+1,3);
              ArCut4[length(ArCut4)-1,0] := Num;
              ArCut4[length(ArCut4)-1,1] := Price1;
              ArCut4[length(ArCut4)-1,2] := Price2;
            end
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//


        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 5 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid5 do
        begin
          //---------------------------5 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            found := false;
            for i := 0 to length(ArCut5)-1 do
            begin
              if  ArCut5[i,0] = Num then
              begin
                found := true;
                break;
              end;
            end;
            if Not found then
            begin
              setlength(ArCut5,length(ArCut5)+1,3);
              ArCut5[length(ArCut5)-1,0] := Num;
              ArCut5[length(ArCut5)-1,1] := Price1;
            end
            else
            begin
              ArCut5[i,1] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut5[i,1],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------5 ตัวโต๊ด ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(LobPos=1) then // (foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
          begin
            found := false;
            for i := 0 to length(ArCut5)-1 do
            begin
              if  ArCut5[i,0] = Num then
              begin
                found := true;
                ArCut5[i,2] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut5[i,2],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
                break;
              end;
            end;
            if Not found then
            begin
              setlength(ArCut5,length(ArCut5)+1,3);
              ArCut5[length(ArCut5)-1,0] := Num;
              ArCut5[length(ArCut5)-1,2] := Price1;
            end
            //else
            //begin
              
            //end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------4 ตัวตรง - โต๊ด --------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(LobPos > 1) then // and(foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
          begin
            found := false;
            for i := 0 to length(ArCut5)-1 do
            begin
              if  ArCut5[i,0] = Num then
              begin
                found := true;
                ArCut5[i,1] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut5[i,1],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
                ArCut5[i,2] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut5[i,2],0)+StrToFloat(Price2));

                break;
              end;
            end;
            if Not found then
            begin
              setlength(ArCut5,length(ArCut5)+1,3);
              ArCut5[length(ArCut5)-1,0] := Num;
              ArCut5[length(ArCut5)-1,1] := Price1;
              ArCut5[length(ArCut5)-1,2] := Price2;
            end
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//


        end; 
      end
      else   //---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------//
      begin

        Price1 := '0';
        Price2 := '0';
        StarPos:= Pos('*',ValDwn);
        LobPos := Pos('-',ValDwn);


        if (Not (foundChar(ValDwn,'*'))) and (Not (foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
        begin
            Price1 := ValDwn;
            Price2 := '0';
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValDwn,'*')) then
        begin
          if StarPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos > 1)and(StarPos < length(ValDwn)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,StarPos+1,length(ValDwn)-StarPos);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos = length(ValDwn)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Price1;
          end;
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValDwn,'-')) then
        begin
          if LobPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
            Price2 := '0';
          end
          else
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,1,LobPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,LobPos+1,length(ValDwn)-LobPos);
          end;
        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 1 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid1 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- วิ่งล่าง ----------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=1) and (StarPos =0) and (LobPos = 0) then //and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if ArCut1[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              ArCut1[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            ArCut1[5,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[5,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหน้าล่าง ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in ['-'])and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if ArCut1[0,StrToInt(Num[1])] = '' then
              ArCut1[0,StrToInt(Num[1])] :=  Num[1];
            ArCut1[6,StrToInt(Num[1])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[6,StrToInt(Num[1])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหลังล่าง -------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['-'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in  ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if ArCut1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] = '' then
              ArCut1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  Num[2];
            ArCut1[7,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[7,StrToInt(Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

        end;


 //---------------------------- เลข 2 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid2 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวล่าง ----------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and (Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง เลชคู่ -----------------//
          if (length(Num)=1)and(Num[1] in ['.']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i);
              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง เลขพี่น้อง -----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['.'])and(Num[2] in ['.']) then
          begin

            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              if i = 9 then
                Num := IntToStr(i)+'0'
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i+1);
              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
                
              ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

            for i := 9 Downto 0 do
            begin
              if i = 0 then
                Num := IntToStr(i)+'9'
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i-1);

              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;

              ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง รูดหน้า-----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['/']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := String(Num[1])+IntToStr(i);
              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง รูดหลัง-----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['/'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(Num[2]);
              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง 19 ประตู----------------//
          if (length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['1'])and(Num[2] in ['9'])and(Num[3] in ['.'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            sNum := Num;
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := String(sNum[4])+IntToStr(i);
              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(sNum[4]);
              if intToStr(i) <> String(sNum[4]) then
              begin
                if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                  ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
                ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
              end;
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวคูณล่าง--------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and ((foundChar(ValDwn,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
           for i := 1 to 2 do
           begin
            if i = 1 then
            begin
              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end
            else
            begin
              if ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] = '' then
                ArCut2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] :=  Num[2]+Num[1];
              ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut2[6,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])],0)+StrToFloat(Price2));
            end;
           end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 3 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid3 do
        begin
          //------------------------- 3 ตัวล่าง 1 ตัว ---------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              ArCut3[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(ArCut3[3,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //-------------------------- 3 ตัวล่างคูณ -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(length(Num)=3)and(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')) then
          begin
                SortNumCut(Num,ValDwn,false);
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//
    

        end;
      end;  
    end;
  end;
end;



procedure TfMain.TranslateCut1(Num,ValUp,ValDwn,ID: String);
Var i,j,k,StarPos,LobPos: integer;
    Price1,Price2,sNum : String;
    found: boolean;
begin

  with Dm do
  begin
    with Numslist,InputGrid do
    begin
      Price1 := '0';
      Price2 := '0';

      if ValUp <> '' then
      begin
        StarPos:= Pos('*',ValUP);
        LobPos := Pos('-',ValUP);

        if (Not (foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not (foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
        begin
            Price1 := ValUp;
            Price2 := '0';
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then
        begin
          if StarPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
          end
          else
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,StarPos+1,length(ValUp)-StarPos);
          end;
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
        begin
          if LobPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
            Price2 := '0';
          end
          else
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,LobPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,LobPos+1,length(ValUp)-LobPos);
          end;
        end;


 //---------------------------- เลข 1 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid1 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- วิ่งบน ----------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(length(Num)=1) and(StarPos =0)and(LobPos = 0) then //    and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array1[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array1[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array1[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array1[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหน้าบน ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in ['-'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[1])] = '' then
              Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[1])] :=  Num[1];
            Array1[2,StrToInt(Num[1])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array1[2,StrToInt(Num[1])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักกลางบน ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['-'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['-'])and((length(Num)=3))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] = '' then
              Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  Num[2];
            Array1[3,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array1[3,StrToInt(Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหลังบน ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['-'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in  ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] = '' then
              Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  Num[2];
            Array1[4,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array1[4,StrToInt(Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//
   
        end;
   
 //---------------------------- เลข 2 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid2 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;

          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน เลชคู่ -----------------//
          if (length(Num)=1)and(Num[1] in ['.']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน เลขพี่น้อง -----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['.'])and(Num[2] in ['.']) then
          begin

            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              if i = 9 then
                Num := IntToStr(i)+'0'
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i+1);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
                
              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

            for i := 9 Downto 0 do
            begin
              if i = 0 then
                Num := IntToStr(i)+'9'
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i-1);

              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;

              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน รูดหน้า-----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['/']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := String(Num[1])+IntToStr(i);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน รูดหลัง-----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['/'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(Num[2]);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวบน 19 ประตู----------------//
          if (length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['1'])and(Num[2] in ['9'])and(Num[3] in ['.'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            sNum := Num;
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := String(sNum[4])+IntToStr(i);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(sNum[4]);
              if intToStr(i) <> String(sNum[4]) then
              begin
                if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                  Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
                Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
              end;
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------2 ตัวโต๊ด --------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Length(Num)=2) and (LobPos = 1)  then
          begin
            if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array2[2,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[2,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวคูณบน--------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and ((foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
           for i := 1 to 2 do
           begin
            if i = 1 then
            begin
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end
            else
            begin
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] :=  Num[2]+Num[1];
              Array2[1,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[1,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[1])],0)+StrToFloat(Price2));
            end;
           end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวมี ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['+']) and (length(Num)=3) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] = '' then
              Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] :=  Num[1]+Num[2];
            Array2[3,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[3,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวหน้า -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['-']) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] = '' then
              Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] :=  Num[1]+Num[2];
            Array2[4,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[4,StrToInt(Num[1]+Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวหลัง -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['-']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9']) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
           if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[3])] = '' then
              Array2[0,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[3])] :=  Num[2]+Num[3];
            Array2[5,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[3])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[5,StrToInt(Num[2]+Num[3])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 3 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid3 do
        begin
  
          //---------------------------3 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------3 ตัวโต๊ด --------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))and(LobPos = 1)  then
          begin
            if Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array3[2,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array3[2,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- 3 ตัวตรง-โต๊ด -----------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))and(LobPos <> 1)  then
          begin
            if Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            Array3[2,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array3[2,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price2));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //-------------------------- 3 ตัวบนคูณ -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then
          begin
                SortNum1(Num,ValUp,True);
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //-------------------------- 4 ตัวบนคูณเป็น 3 ตัว -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and (Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then
          begin
                Sort4Num(Num,ValUp);

                for j := Items.Count-1 Downto 0 do
                begin
                  if Array3[0,StrToInt(Items[j].Caption)] = '' then
                    Array3[0,StrToInt(Items[j].Caption)] := Items[j].Caption;
                  Array3[1,StrToInt(Items[j].Caption)] := FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array3[1,StrToInt(Items[j].Caption)],0)+StrToFloat(Items[j].SubItems[0]));
                end;
              
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//


        end;


 //---------------------------- เลข 4 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with CutGrid4 do
        begin
          //---------------------------4 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if CutGrid4[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  CutGrid4[1,k] := floattoStr(StrTofloatDef(CutGrid4[1,k],0)+StrtoFloatDef(Price1,0));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  CutGrid4[0,row] := Num;
                  CutGrid4[1,row] := Price1;
              end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------4 ตัวโต๊ด ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if CutGrid4[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  CutGrid4[2,k] := floattoStr(StrTofloatDef(CutGrid4[2,k],0)+StrtoFloatDef(Price1,0));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  CutGrid4[0,row] := Num;
                  CutGrid4[2,row] := Price1;
              end;

           {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              CutGrid4[0,row] := Num;
              CutGrid4[2,row] := Price1;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 5 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with CutGrid5 do
        begin
          //---------------------------5 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if CutGrid5[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  CutGrid5[1,k] := floattoStr(StrTofloatDef(CutGrid5[1,k],0)+StrtoFloatDef(Price1,0));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  CutGrid5[0,row] := Num;
                  CutGrid5[1,row] := Price1;
              end;
          {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              CutGrid5[0,row] := Num;
              CutGrid5[1,row] := Price1;
            end;
           }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------5 ตัวโต๊ด ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if CutGrid5[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  CutGrid5[2,k] := floattoStr(StrTofloatDef(CutGrid5[2,k],0)+StrtoFloatDef(Price1,0));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  CutGrid5[0,row] := Num;
                  CutGrid5[2,row] := Price1;
              end;
          {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              CutGrid5[0,row] := Num;
              CutGrid5[2,row] := Price1;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

        end;



      end
      else   //---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------//
      begin

        Price1 := '0';
        Price2 := '0';
        StarPos:= Pos('*',ValDwn);
        LobPos := Pos('-',ValDwn);


        if (Not (foundChar(ValDwn,'*'))) and (Not (foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
        begin
            Price1 := ValDwn;
            Price2 := '0';
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValDwn,'*')) then
        begin
          if StarPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
          end
          else
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,StarPos+1,length(ValDwn)-StarPos);
          end;
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValDwn,'-')) then
        begin
          if LobPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
            Price2 := '0';
          end
          else
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,1,LobPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,LobPos+1,length(ValDwn)-LobPos);
          end;
        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 1 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid1 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- วิ่งล่าง ----------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=1) and (StarPos =0) and (LobPos = 0) then //and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array1[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array1[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array1[5,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array1[5,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหน้าล่าง ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in ['-'])and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[1])] = '' then
              Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[1])] :=  Num[1];
            Array1[6,StrToInt(Num[1])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array1[6,StrToInt(Num[1])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหลังล่าง -------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['-'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in  ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] = '' then
              Array1[0,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  Num[2];
            Array1[7,StrToInt(Num[2])] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array1[7,StrToInt(Num[2])],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

        end;


 //---------------------------- เลข 2 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid2 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวล่าง ----------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and (Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง เลชคู่ -----------------//
          if (length(Num)=1)and(Num[1] in ['.']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง เลขพี่น้อง -----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['.'])and(Num[2] in ['.']) then
          begin

            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              if i = 9 then
                Num := IntToStr(i)+'0'
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i+1);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
                
              Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

            for i := 9 Downto 0 do
            begin
              if i = 0 then
                Num := IntToStr(i)+'9'
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i-1);

              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;

              Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง รูดหน้า-----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['/']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := String(Num[1])+IntToStr(i);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง รูดหลัง-----------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['/'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(Num[2]);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //----------------------- เลขชุด 2 ตัวล่าง 19 ประตู----------------//
          if (length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['1'])and(Num[2] in ['9'])and(Num[3] in ['.'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            sNum := Num;
            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := String(sNum[4])+IntToStr(i);
              if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
              Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
            end;

            for i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(sNum[4]);
              if intToStr(i) <> String(sNum[4]) then
              begin
                if Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
                  Array2[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
                Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array2[6,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
              end;
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------// 

        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 3 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid3 do
        begin
          //------------------------- 3 ตัวล่าง 1 ตัว ---------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            if Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] = '' then
              Array3[0,StrToInt(Num)] :=  Num;
            Array3[3,StrToInt(Num)] :=  FormatFloat('##0.##',StrToFloatDef(Array3[3,StrToInt(Num)],0)+StrToFloat(Price1));
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //-------------------------- 3 ตัวล่างคูณ -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(length(Num)=3)and(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')) then
          begin
                SortNum1(Num,ValDwn,false);
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//
    

        end;
      end;  
    end;
   
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.Translate(Num,ValUp,ValDwn,ID: String);
Var i,j,k,StarPos,LobPos: integer;
    Price1,Price2,Nums : String;
    found: boolean;
begin

  with Dm do
  begin
    NiceGrid1.BeginUpdate;
    NiceGrid2.BeginUpdate;
    NiceGrid3.BeginUpdate;

    with Numslist,InputGrid do
    begin
      Price1 := '0';
      Price2 := '0';
      if ValUp <> '' then
      begin
        StarPos:= Pos('*',ValUP);
        LobPos := Pos('-',ValUP);

        if (Not (foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not (foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
        begin
            Price1 := ValUp;
            Price2 := '0';
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then
        begin
          if StarPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos > 1)and(StarPos < length(Valup)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,StarPos+1,length(ValUp)-StarPos);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos = length(Valup)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Price1;
          end;
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
        begin
          if LobPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
            Price2 := '0';
          end
          else
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,LobPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,LobPos+1,length(ValUp)-LobPos);
          end;
        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 1 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid1 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- วิ่งบน ----------------------------//
          if (length(Num)=1)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and(StarPos =0)and(LobPos = 0) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid1[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid1[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[1,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid1[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid1[1,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid1[8,row] := ID;
              end;
         {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid1[0,row] := Num;
              NiceGrid1[1,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid1[8,row] := ID;
            end;
          }

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหน้าบน ---------------------------//
          if  (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['-'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid1[0,k] = Copy(Num,1,1) then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid1[2,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[2,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid1[0,row] := Copy(Num,1,1);
                  NiceGrid1[2,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid1[8,row] := ID;
              end;

          {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid1[0,row] := Copy(Num,1,1);
              NiceGrid1[2,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid1[8,row] := ID;
            end;
         }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักกลางบน ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['-'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['-'])and((length(Num)=3))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid1[0,k] = Copy(Num,2,1) then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid1[3,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[3,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid1[0,row] := Copy(Num,2,1);
                  NiceGrid1[3,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid1[8,row] := ID;
              end;
          {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid1[0,row] := Copy(Num,2,1);
              NiceGrid1[3,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid1[8,row] := ID;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหลังบน ---------------------------//
          if  (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['-'])and(Num[2] in  ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid1[0,k] = Copy(Num,2,1) then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid1[4,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[4,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid1[0,row] := Copy(Num,2,1);
                  NiceGrid1[4,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid1[8,row] := ID;
              end;
          {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid1[0,row] := Copy(Num,2,1);
              NiceGrid1[4,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid1[8,row] := ID;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//
   
        end;
   

 //---------------------------- เลข 2 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid2 do
        begin

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(StarPos = 0)and(LobPos = 0) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
           {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
              NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวบนเลขชุด เลขคู่ ---------------------//
          if (length(Num)=1)and(Num[1] in ['.']) then
          begin
            For i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              found := false;
              Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวบนเลขชุด พี่น้อง ---------------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['.'])and(Num[2] in ['.']) then
          begin
            For i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              found := false;
              if i < 9 then
                Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i+1)
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i)+'0';
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
            end;
            For i := 9 Downto 0 do
            begin
              found := false;
              if i = 9 then
                Num := '0'+IntToStr(i)
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i+1)+IntToStr(i);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวบนเลขชุด รูดหน้า---------------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['/'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            For i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              found := false;
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(Num[2]);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวบนเลขชุด รูดหลัง---------------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['/']) then
          begin
            For i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              found := false;
              Num := String(Num[1])+IntToStr(i);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวบนเลขชุด หาง 19 ประตู-----------------//
          if (length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['1'])and(Num[2] in ['9'])and(Num[3] in ['.'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            Nums := Num;
            For i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              found := false;
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(Nums[4]);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
            end;

            For i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
             found := false;
             if IntToSTr(i) <> String(Nums[4]) then
             begin
                Num := String(Nums[4])+IntToStr(i);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
             end;
            end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวคูณบน--------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and ((foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
           for i := 1 to 2 do
           begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  if i = 1 then
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])+txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])+txtToFloat(Price2));

                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  if i = 1 then
                    NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price1
                  else
                    NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price2;

                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
           {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
              if i = 1 then
                NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price1
              else
                NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price2;
              NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
            end;
            }
            Num := String(Num[2])+String(Num[1]);
           end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//
    
          //---------------------------2 ตัวโต๊ด --------------------------//
          if  (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1]in['0'..'9'])and(Num[2]in['0'..'9'])and(LobPos = 1) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[2,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[2,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[2,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
          {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
              NiceGrid2[2,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวบน-โต๊ด ----------------------//
          if  (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and (LobPos > 1) and (LobPos < length(ValUp)) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  NiceGrid2[2,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[2,k])+txtToFloat(Price2));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[2,row] := Price2;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
          {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
              NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid2[2,row] := Price2;
              NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวมี ---------------------------//
          if  (length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['+'])and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Copy(Num,1,2) then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[3,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[3,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Copy(Num,1,2);
                  NiceGrid2[3,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
          {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid2[0,row] := Copy(Num,1,2);
              NiceGrid2[3,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวหน้า -------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['-']) and (length(Num)=3) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Copy(Num,1,2) then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[4,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[4,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Copy(Num,1,2);
                  NiceGrid2[4,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
          {
            end
            else
            begin

          if (Num[1] in ['-']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=3) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then

            end
            else

                  NiceGrid3[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid3[2,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid3[4,row] := ID;
              end;
         {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid3[0,row] := Num;
              NiceGrid3[2,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid3[4,row] := ID;
            end;
          }

          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- 3 ตัวตรง-โต๊ด -----------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))and(LobPos <> 1)  then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then

                  found := true;

                  NiceGrid3[2,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid3[2,k])+txtToFloat(Price2));

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid3[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid3[1,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid3[2,row] := Price2;
                  NiceGrid3[4,row] := ID;
              end;
         {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid3[0,row] := Num;
              NiceGrid3[1,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid3[2,row] := Price2;
              NiceGrid3[4,row] := ID;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //-------------------------- 3 ตัวบนคูณ -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
                SortNum2(Num,ValUp);
                for j := Items.Count-1 Downto 0 do
                begin

                  found := false;
                  for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
                  begin
                    if NiceGrid3[0,k] = Items[j].Caption then
                    begin
                      found := true;
                      NiceGrid3[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid3[1,k])+txtToFloat(Items[j].SubItems[0]));
                      break;

                    end;
                  end;
                  if Not found then
                  begin
                    if RowCount = 0 then
                      RowCount := 1
                    else
                      InsertRow(0);
                      
                    NiceGrid3[0,0] := Items[j].Caption;
                    NiceGrid3[1,0] := Items[j].SubItems[0];
                    NiceGrid3[4,0] := ID;

                  end;
                end;
          {

              for j := Items.Count-1 Downto 0 do

                  RowCount := 1
                else
                  InsertRow(0);
                NiceGrid3[0,0] := Items[j].Caption;
                NiceGrid3[1,0] := Items[j].SubItems[0];
                NiceGrid3[4,0] := ID;

              end;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //-------------------- 4 ตัวบนคูณเป็น 3 ตัว ---------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and (Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then
          begin

                  begin

                    else

                NiceGrid3[1,0] := Items[j].SubItems[0];
                NiceGrid3[4,0] := ID;

        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 4 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid4 do

          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then

                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid4[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid4[2,row] := Price1;
              end;
          {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid4[0,row] := Num;
              NiceGrid4[2,row] := Price1;
            end;
          }

          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))and(LobPos > 1)  then

                  NiceGrid4[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid4[1,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid4[2,row] := Price2;
                  NiceGrid4[3,row] := ID;
              end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//


        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 5 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid5 do
        begin
          //---------------------------5 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then

          {

            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid5[0,row] := Num;
              NiceGrid5[1,row] := Price1;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //---------------------------5 ตัวโต๊ด ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(LobPos = 1) then // and(foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid5[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid5[2,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid5[2,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;

              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid5[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid5[2,row] := Price1;
              end;
          {
            end
            else
