              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid5[0,row] := Num;
              NiceGrid5[2,row] := Price1;

          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- 4 ตัวตรง-โต๊ด -----------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))and(LobPos <> 1)  then
          begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid5[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid5[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid5[1,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  NiceGrid5[2,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid5[2,k])+txtToFloat(Price2));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid5[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid5[1,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid5[2,row] := Price2;
                  NiceGrid5[3,row] := ID;
              end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

        end;
      end
      else   //---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------//
      begin

        Price1 := '0';
        Price2 := '0';
        StarPos:= Pos('*',ValDwn);
        LobPos := Pos('-',ValDwn);


        if (Not (foundChar(ValDwn,'*'))) and (Not (foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
        begin
            Price1 := ValDwn;
            Price2 := '0';
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValDwn,'*')) then
        begin
          if StarPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos > 1)and(StarPos < length(ValDwn)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,StarPos+1,length(ValDwn)-StarPos);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos = length(ValDwn)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Price1;
          end;
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValDwn,'-')) then
        begin
          if LobPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
            Price2 := '0';
          end
          else
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,1,LobPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,LobPos+1,length(ValDwn)-LobPos);
          end;
        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 1 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid1 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- วิ่งล่าง ----------------------------//

          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=1) and (StarPos =0) and (LobPos = 0) then //and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid1[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid1[5,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[5,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid1[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid1[5,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid1[8,row] := ID;
              end;
          {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid1[0,row] := Num;
              NiceGrid1[5,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid1[8,row] := ID;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหน้าล่าง ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in ['-'])and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid1[0,k] = Copy(Num,1,1) then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid1[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[6,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid1[0,row] := Copy(Num,1,1);
                  NiceGrid1[6,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid1[8,row] := ID;
              end;
         {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid1[0,row] := Copy(Num,1,1);
              NiceGrid1[6,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid1[8,row] := ID;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหลังล่าง -------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['-'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in  ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid1[0,k] = Copy(Num,2,1) then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid1[7,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[7,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid1[0,row] := Copy(Num,2,1);
                  NiceGrid1[7,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid1[8,row] := ID;
              end;
          {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid1[0,row] := Copy(Num,2,1);
              NiceGrid1[7,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid1[8,row] := ID;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 2 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid2 do
        begin
          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวล่าง -------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and (Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin

              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));

                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[6,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
          {
            end
            else

              NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
              NiceGrid2[6,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวล่างเลขชุด เลขคู่ ---------------------//
          if (length(Num)=1)and(Num[1] in ['.']) then
          begin
            For i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              found := false;
              Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[6,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวล่างเลขชุด พี่น้อง ---------------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['.'])and(Num[2] in ['.']) then
          begin
            For i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              found := false;
              if i < 9 then
                Num := IntToStr(i)+IntToStr(i+1)
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i)+'0';
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[6,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
            end;

            For i := 9 Downto 0 do
            begin
              found := false;
              if i = 9 then
                Num := '0'+IntToStr(i)
              else
                Num := IntToStr(i+1)+IntToStr(i);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[6,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวล่างเลขชุด รูดหน้า---------------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['/'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            For i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              found := false;
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(Num[2]);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[6,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวล่างเลขชุด รูดหลัง---------------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['/']) then
          begin
            For i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              found := false;
              Num := String(Num[1])+IntToStr(i);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[6,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
            end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวล่างเลขชุด หาง 19 ประตู-----------------//
          if (length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['1'])and(Num[2] in ['9'])and(Num[3] in ['.'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
            Nums := Num;
            For i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
              found := false;
              Num := IntToStr(i)+String(Nums[4]);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[6,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
            end;

            For i := 0 to 9 do
            begin
             found := false;
             if IntToSTr(i) <> String(Nums[4]) then
             begin
                Num := String(Nums[4])+IntToStr(i);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid2[6,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
             end;
            end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//


          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวคูณล่าง--------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and ((foundChar(ValDwn,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
           for i := 1 to 2 do
           begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  if i = 1 then
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])+txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])+txtToFloat(Price2));

                  break;
                end;
              end;

              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
                  if i = 1 then
                    NiceGrid2[6,row] := Price1
                  else
                    NiceGrid2[6,row] := Price2;

                  NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
              end;
           {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid2[0,row] := Num;
              if i = 1 then
                NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price1
              else
                NiceGrid2[1,row] := Price2;
              NiceGrid2[7,row] := ID;
            end;
            }
            Num := String(Num[2])+String(Num[1]);
           end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//
        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 3 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid3 do
        begin
          //------------------------- 3 ตัวล่าง 1 ตัว ---------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
              found := false;
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid3[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  found := true;
                  NiceGrid3[3,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid3[3,k])+txtToFloat(Price1));
                  break;
                end;
              end;
              if Not found then
              begin
                  AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
                  NiceGrid3[0,row] := Num;
                  NiceGrid3[3,row] := Price1;
                  NiceGrid3[4,row] := ID;
              end;
          {
            end
            else
            begin
              AddRow; row := rowcount-1;
              NiceGrid3[0,row] := Num;
              NiceGrid3[3,row] := Price1;
              NiceGrid3[4,row] := ID;
            end;
          }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //-------------------------- 3 ตัวล่างคูณ -------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Length(Num)=3)and(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')) then
          begin
            //if ChkSum.Checked then
            //begin
                SortNum2(Num,ValDwn);
                for j := Items.Count-1 Downto 0 do
                begin

                  found := false;
                  for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
                  begin
                    if NiceGrid3[0,k] = Items[j].Caption then
                    begin
                      found := true;
                      NiceGrid3[3,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid3[3,k])+txtToFloat(Items[j].SubItems[0]));
                      break;
                    end;
                  end;
                  if Not found then
                  begin
                    if Rowcount = 0 then
                      RowCount := 1
                    else
                      InsertRow(0);
                    NiceGrid3[0,0] := Items[j].Caption;
                    NiceGrid3[3,0] := Items[j].SubItems[0];
                    NiceGrid3[4,0] := ID;
                  end;
                end;
           {
            end
            else
            begin
              SortNum(Num,ValDwn);
              for j := Items.Count-1 Downto 0 do
              begin
                if Rowcount = 0 then
                  RowCount := 1
                else
                  InsertRow(0);
                NiceGrid3[0,0] := Items[j].Caption;
                NiceGrid3[3,0] := Items[j].SubItems[0];
                NiceGrid3[4,0] := ID;
              end;
            
            end;
           }
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//  

        end;
      end;  
    end;
    NiceGrid1.EndUpdate;
    NiceGrid2.EndUpdate;
    NiceGrid3.EndUpdate;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.DelTranslate(Num,ValUp,ValDwn,ID: String);
Var i,j,k,StarPos,LobPos: integer;
    Price,Price1,Price2,sNum : String;
    found: boolean;
    ColVal: Array of Real;
begin

  with Dm do
  begin
    NiceGrid1.BeginUpdate;
    NiceGrid2.BeginUpdate;
    NiceGrid3.BeginUpdate;

    with Numslist,InputGrid do
    begin
      Price1 := '0';
      Price2 := '0';

      if ValUp <> '' then
      begin
        StarPos:= Pos('*',ValUP);
        LobPos := Pos('-',ValUP);

        if (Not (foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not (foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
        begin
            Price1 := ValUp;
            Price2 := '0';
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then
        begin
          if StarPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos > 1)and(StarPos < length(ValUp)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,StarPos+1,length(ValUp)-StarPos);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos = length(ValUp)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Price1;
          end;
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
        begin
          if LobPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,2,length(ValUp)-1);
            Price2 := '0';
          end
          else
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValUp,1,LobPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValUp,LobPos+1,length(ValUp)-LobPos);
          end;
        end;


 //---------------------------- เลข 1 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid1 do
        begin
          Setlength(ColVal,ColCount-2);
          //--------------------------- วิ่งบน ----------------------------//
          if (length(Num)=1)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(StarPos =0)and(LobPos = 0) then
          begin
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid1[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[1,k]) then
                    NiceGrid1[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[1,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid1[1,k] := '';

                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0)and(ColVal[6]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  break;
                end;
              end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหน้าบน ---------------------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['-'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid1[0,k] = Copy(Num,1,1) then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[2,k]) then
                    NiceGrid1[2,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[2,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid1[2,k] := '';

                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0)and(ColVal[6]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  break;
                end;
              end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักกลางบน ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['-'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['-'])and((length(Num)=3))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid1[0,k] = Copy(Num,2,1) then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[3,k]) then
                    NiceGrid1[3,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[3,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid1[3,k] := '';

                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0)and(ColVal[6]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  break;
                end;
              end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหลังบน ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['-'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in  ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid1[0,k] = Copy(Num,2,1) then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[4,k]) then
                    NiceGrid1[4,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[4,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid1[4,k] := '';

                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0)and(ColVal[6]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  break;
                end;
              end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//
   
        end;
   

 //---------------------------- เลข 2 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid2 do
        begin
          Setlength(ColVal,ColCount-2);
          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวบน ----------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  break;
                end;
              end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวคูณบน--------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and ((foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
           for j := 1 to 2 do
           begin
              Price := Price1;
              if J = 2 then
              begin
                Num := Num[2]+Num[1];
                Price := Price2;
              end;

              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])-txtToFloat(Price))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
           end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวบนเลขชุด เลขคู่ ---------------------//
          if (length(Num)=1)and(Num[1] in ['.']) then
          begin
           for j := 0 to 9 do
           begin
              Num := IntToStr(j)+IntToStr(j);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
           end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวบนเลขชุด เลขพี่น้อง-------------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['.'])and(Num[2] in ['.']) then
          begin
           for j := 0 to 9 do
           begin
              if j < 9 then
                Num := IntToStr(j)+IntToStr(j+1)
              else
                Num := IntToStr(j)+'0';
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
           end;

           for j := 0 to 9 do
           begin
              if j = 9 then
                Num := '0'+IntToStr(j)
              else
                Num := IntToStr(j+1)+IntToStr(j);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
           end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวบนเลขชุด รูดหน้า ---------------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['/']) then
          begin
           for j := 0 to 9 do
           begin
              Num := String(Num[1])+IntToStr(j);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
           end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวบนเลขชุด รูดหลัง ---------------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['/'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
           for j := 0 to 9 do
           begin
              Num := IntToStr(j)+String(Num[2]);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
           end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวบนเลขชุด หาง 19 ประตู ----------------//
          if (length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['1'])and(Num[2] in ['9'])and(Num[3] in ['.'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
           sNum := Num;
           for j := 0 to 9 do
           begin
              Num := IntToStr(j)+String(sNum[4]);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
           end;

           for j := 0 to 9 do
           begin
             if IntToSTr(j) <> String(sNum[4]) then
             begin
                Num := String(sNum[4])+IntToStr(j);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
             end;
           end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

    
          //---------------------------2 ตัวโต๊ด --------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Length(Num)=2) and (LobPos = 1)  then
          begin
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[2,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[2,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[2,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[2,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  break;
                end;
              end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวบน-โต๊ด ----------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and (LobPos > 1) and (LobPos < length(ValUp)) then
          begin
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k]) then
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[1,k])-txtToFloat(Price1));
                    NiceGrid2[2,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[2,k])-txtToFloat(Price2));
                  end
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[1,k] := '';
                    NiceGrid2[2,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  break;
                end;
              end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวมี ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['+']) and (length(Num)=3) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Copy(Num,1,2) then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[3,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[3,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[3,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[3,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  break;
                end;
              end;
          end;

          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['-']) and (length(Num)=3) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then
          begin

          begin

 //---------------------------- เลข 3 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//

          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))and(LobPos <> 1)  then
          begin

              begin
                if NiceGrid3[0,k] = Num then

                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid3[1,k]) then
                  begin
                    NiceGrid3[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid3[1,k])-txtToFloat(Price1));
                    NiceGrid3[2,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid3[2,k])-txtToFloat(Price2));
                  end

                    NiceGrid3[2,k] := '';

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);

                  break;
                end;

          //-------------------------- 3 ตัวบนคูณ -------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=3)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then
          begin
            SortNum2(Num,ValUp);
            for j := 1 to Items.Count do
            begin
              Num := Items[j-1].Caption;
              Price := Items[J-1].SubItems[0];
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid3[0,k] = Num then
                begin

                      DeleteRow(K);

                  Break;

          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and (Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'*')) then

          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then

                    NiceGrid4[1,k] := formatfloat('###,###.##',(txtToFloat(NiceGrid4[1,k])-txtToFloat(Price1)));

                  if (txtToFloat(NiceGrid4[2,k])-txtToFloat(Price2)) >= 0 then
                    NiceGrid4[2,k] := formatfloat('###,###.##',(txtToFloat(NiceGrid4[2,k])-txtToFloat(Price2)));

                  if (NiceGrid4[1,k] <> '')or(NiceGrid4[1,k] <> '') then
                    break
                  else
                    DeleteRow(k);

                end;
              end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

       {

          //---------------------------4 ตัวตรง-โต๊ด ----------------------------//
          if (Length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(LobPos>1) then //and(foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then
          begin
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid4[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid4[2,k]) then
                  begin
                    NiceGrid4[1,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid4[1,k])-txtToFloat(Price1));
                    NiceGrid4[2,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid4[2,k])-txtToFloat(Price2));
                  end

 //---------------------------- เลข 5 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid5 do

          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))) then

          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(foundChar(ValUp,'-'))and(LobPos > 1)  then
          begin

          if (Length(Num)=5)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[5] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValUp,'*')))and(LobPos>1) then // (foundChar(ValUp,'-')) then

        }

        end;

      end
      else   //---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------//
      begin

        Price1 := '0';
        Price2 := '0';
        StarPos:= Pos('*',ValDwn);
        LobPos := Pos('-',ValDwn);


        if (Not (foundChar(ValDwn,'*'))) and (Not (foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
        begin
            Price1 := ValDwn;
            Price2 := '0';
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValDwn,'*')) then
        begin
          if StarPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos > 1)and(StarPos < length(ValDwn)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,StarPos+1,length(ValDwn)-StarPos);
          end
          else
          if (StarPos = length(ValDwn)) then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,1,StarPos-1);
            Price2 := Price1;
          end;
        end
        else
        if (foundChar(ValDwn,'-')) then
        begin
          if LobPos = 1 then
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,2,length(ValDwn)-1);
            Price2 := '0';
          end
          else
          begin
            Price1 := Copy(ValDwn,1,LobPos-1);
            Price2 := Copy(ValDwn,LobPos+1,length(ValDwn)-LobPos);
          end;
        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 1 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid1 do
        begin
          Setlength(ColVal,ColCount-2);

                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[5,k]) then
                    NiceGrid1[5,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[5,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid1[5,k] := '';

                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0)and(ColVal[6]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  break;
                end;
              end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------------- ปักหน้าล่าง ---------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(length(Num)=2)and(Num[2] in ['-'])and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do

                    NiceGrid1[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid1[6,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))

                      DeleteRow(K);

 //---------------------------- เลข 2 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//

          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and (Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then

          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //--------------------------- 2 ตัวคูณล่าง ----------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(Num)=2) and ((foundChar(ValDwn,'*'))) and (Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
           for j := 1 to 2 do
           begin
              Price := Price1;
              if J = 2 then
              begin
                Num := Num[2]+Num[1];
                Price := Price2;
              end;

              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])-txtToFloat(Price))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
           end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวล่างเลขชุด เลขคู่ ---------------------//
          if (length(Num)=1)and(Num[1] in ['.']) then
          begin
           for j := 0 to 9 do
           begin
              Num := IntToStr(j)+IntToStr(j);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
           end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวล่างเลขชุด เลขพี่น้อง-------------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['.'])and(Num[2] in ['.']) then
          begin
           for j := 0 to 9 do
           begin
              if j < 9 then
                Num := IntToStr(j)+IntToStr(j+1)
              else
                Num := IntToStr(j)+'0';
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
           end;

           for j := 0 to 9 do
           begin
              if j = 9 then
                Num := '0'+IntToStr(j)
              else
                Num := IntToStr(j+1)+IntToStr(j);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
           end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวล่างเลขชุด รูดหน้า ---------------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['0'..'9'])and(Num[2] in ['/']) then
          begin
           for j := 0 to 9 do
           begin
              Num := String(Num[1])+IntToStr(j);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
           end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวล่างเลขชุด รูดหลัง ---------------------//
          if (length(Num)=2)and(Num[1] in ['/'])and(Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
           for j := 0 to 9 do
           begin
              Num := IntToStr(j)+String(Num[2]);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
           end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //------------------- 2 ตัวล่างเลขชุด หาง 19 ประตู ----------------//
          if (length(Num)=4)and(Num[1] in ['1'])and(Num[2] in ['9'])and(Num[3] in ['.'])and(Num[4] in ['0'..'9']) then
          begin
           sNum := Num;
           for j := 0 to 9 do
           begin
              Num := IntToStr(j)+String(sNum[4]);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
           end;

           for j := 0 to 9 do
           begin
             if IntToSTr(j) <> String(sNum[4]) then
             begin
                Num := String(sNum[4])+IntToStr(j);
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid2[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k]) then
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[6,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid2[6,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid2[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0)and(ColVal[3]=0)and(ColVal[4]=0)and(ColVal[5]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
             end;
           end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//
          
        end;

 //---------------------------- เลข 3 ตัว ---------------------------------------------------//
        with NiceGrid3 do
        begin
          Setlength(ColVal,ColCount-2);
          //------------------------- 3 ตัวล่าง 1 ตัว ---------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')))and(Not(foundChar(ValDwn,'-'))) then
          begin
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid3[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price1) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid3[3,k]) then
                    NiceGrid3[3,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid3[3,k])-txtToFloat(Price1))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid3[3,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtToFloat(NiceGrid3[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  break;
                end;
              end;
          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//

          //-------------------------- 3 ตัวล่างคูณ -------------------------//
          if (Num[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (Num[3] in ['0'..'9'])and(Length(Num)=3)and(foundChar(ValDwn,'*')) then
          begin
            SortNum2(Num,ValDwn);
            for j := 1 to Items.Count do
            begin
              Num := Items[j-1].Caption;
              Price := Items[J-1].SubItems[0];
              for k := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
              begin
                if NiceGrid3[0,k] = Num then
                begin
                  if StrtoFloat(Price) < txtToFloat(NiceGrid3[3,k]) then
                    NiceGrid3[3,k] := floattoStr(txtToFloat(NiceGrid3[3,k])-txtToFloat(Price))
                  else
                  begin
                    NiceGrid3[3,k] := '';
                    for i := 1 to ColCount-2 do
                      ColVal[i-1] :=  txtTofloat(NiceGrid3[i,k]);

                    if (ColVal[0]=0)and(ColVal[1]=0)and(ColVal[2]=0) then
                      DeleteRow(K);
                  end;
                  Break;
                end;
              end;
            end;

          end;
          //---------------------------------------------------------------//  


        end;
      end;  
    end;
    NiceGrid1.EndUpdate;
    NiceGrid2.EndUpdate;
    NiceGrid3.EndUpdate;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.edValueKeyDown(Sender: TObject; var Key: Word;
  Shift: TShiftState);
begin
  if Key = VK_Up then
  begin
    EdNumber.SetFocus;
    Key := 0;
  end
  else
  if Key = VK_Down then
  begin
    EdDwnNumber.SetFocus;
    Key := 0;
  end;

  if Key = VK_Escape then
  begin
    ChkEdit.Checked := false;
    if isUp then
      edNumber.SetFocus
    else
      edDwnNumber.SetFocus;
    Key := 0;
  end;  

end;

procedure TfMain.FormShow(Sender: TObject);
Var QrLotType: TABSQuery;
    i: integer;
begin
  edUsID.Text := UserID;
  PnUsName.Caption := ' '+UserName;

  Application.ProcessMessages;
  With Dm do
  begin
    QrLotType := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
    QrLotType.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
    QrLotType.Close;
    QrLotType.SQL.Clear;
    QrlotType.SQL.Add('Select * from Lotto Order By ID');
    QrlotType.Open;

    MainPageControl.TabIndex := 0;
    ComboLotType.Clear;
    QrLotType.First;
    while not QrLotType.Eof do
    begin
      ComboLotType.Items.Add(QrLotType.fieldByName('LotName').AsString);
      QrLotType.Next;
    end;
    ComboLotType.ItemIndex := 0;
    QrLotType.Free;
  end;
  EdCustNo.SetFocus;
  DatePick.Date := Date;
  RefreshBtnClick(Sender);   
end;

procedure TfMain.Look;
Var i: integer;
begin
  with Dm,InputGrid do
  begin
    if ChkLook.Checked then
    begin
      QrData.Close;
      QrData.SQL.Clear;
      if isup then
      begin
        QrData.SQL.Add('Select * from data');
        QrData.SQL.Add('Where (Num like "%'+EdNumber.text+'%") and (Up like "%'+EdValue.text+'%")');
        QrData.SQL.Add('and (LotType = "'+edLotID.Text+'")');
        QrData.SQL.Add('and (Period_Date ="'+DateToStr(Datepick.Date)+'")');
        if Not ChkSumBook.Checked then
          QrData.SQL.Add('and (RefNo = "'+edRefNo.Text +'")');
        if Not ChkSumCust.Checked then
          QrData.SQL.Add('and (CustNo = "'+edCustNo.Text+'")');

        if Not ChkSortInput.Checked then
          QrData.SQL.Add('Order By ID DESC')
        else
          QrData.SQL.Add('Order By ID ASC');

      end
      else
      begin
        QrData.SQL.Add('Select * from data');
        QrData.SQL.Add('Where (Num like "%'+EdDwnNumber.text+'%") and (Dwn like "%'+EdValue.text+'%")');
        QrData.SQL.Add('and (LotType = "'+edLotID.Text+'")');
        QrData.SQL.Add('and (Period_Date ="'+DateToStr(Datepick.Date)+'")');
        if Not ChkSumBook.Checked then
          QrData.SQL.Add('and (RefNo = "'+edRefNo.Text +'")');
        if Not ChkSumCust.Checked then
          QrData.SQL.Add('and (CustNo = "'+edCustNo.Text+'")');

        if Not ChkSortInput.Checked then
          QrData.SQL.Add('Order By ID DESC')
        else
          QrData.SQL.Add('Order By ID ASC');
      end;
      QrData.Open;

      RowCount := 0;
      QrData.First;
      BeginUpDate;
      While Not QrData.Eof do
      begin
        AddRow;

        InputGrid[0,RowCount-1] := QrData.fieldByName('Num').AsString;
        InputGrid[1,RowCount-1] := QrData.fieldByName('Up').AsString;
        InputGrid[2,RowCount-1] := QrData.fieldByName('Dwn').AsString;
        InputGrid[3,RowCount-1] := QrData.fieldByName('ID').AsString;

        QrData.Next;
      end;
      EndUpDate;
      //ProcessBtnClick(self);
      //Progressbar.Progress := 0;
    end
    else
    begin
      QrData.Close;
      QrData.SQL.Clear;
      QrData.SQL.Add('Select * from data');
        QrData.SQL.Add('Where (Period_Date ="'+DateToStr(Datepick.Date)+'")');
        QrData.SQL.Add('and (LotType = "'+edLotID.Text+'")');
        QrData.SQL.Add('and (RefNo = "'+edRefNo.Text+'")');
        QrData.SQL.Add('and (CustNo = "'+edCustNo.Text+'")');
      QrData.SQL.Add('Order by ID Desc');
      QrData.Open;

      RowCount := 0;
      QrData.First;
      BeginUpdate;
      While Not QrData.Eof do
      begin
        AddRow;

        InputGrid[0,RowCount-1] := QrData.fieldByName('Num').AsString;
        InputGrid[1,RowCount-1] := QrData.fieldByName('Up').AsString;
        InputGrid[2,RowCount-1] := QrData.fieldByName('Dwn').AsString;
        InputGrid[3,RowCount-1] := QrData.fieldByName('ID').AsString;

        QrData.Next;
      end;
      EndUpDate;
      
    end;
  end;
  lbItems1.Caption := IntToStr(inputGrid.RowCount)+' รายการ';
  ProcessBtnClick(self);
end;


procedure TfMain.ChkLookChange(Sender: TObject);
begin
  FindInputBtn.Visible := ChkLook.Checked;
  if Not ChkLook.Checked then
    RefreshBtnClick(Sender);
end;

procedure TfMain.find3Click(Sender: TObject);
Var i: integer;
    fNum,Num: String;
begin
 ProcessBtnClick(Sender);
 if edFind3.Text <> '' then
 begin
  for i := NiceGrid3.RowCount-1 Downto 0 do
  begin
    fNum := edFind3.Text;
    Num  := NiceGrid3[0,i];
    if (fNum[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (fNum[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (fNum[3] in ['0'..'9']) and (fNum <> Num)  then
      NiceGrid3.DeleteRow(i);

    if (fNum[1] in ['*']) and (fNum[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (length(fNum)=2) and (fNum[2] <> Num[3])  then
      NiceGrid3.DeleteRow(i);

    if (fNum[1] in ['0'..'9']) and (fNum[2] in ['*']) and (length(fNum)=2) and (fNum[1] <> Num[1])  then
      NiceGrid3.DeleteRow(i);

    if (fNum[1] in ['*']) and (fNum[2] in ['0'..'9']) and (fNum[3] in ['*']) and (fNum[2] <> Num[2])  then
      NiceGrid3.DeleteRow(i);
  end;
 end
end;

procedure TfMain.SortGrid(NGrid: TNiceGrid);
var
  CountItem, I, J, K, L, ThePosition: integer;
begin
  CountItem := NGrid.RowCount;
  with Sortlist do
  begin
      Clear;
      for I := 0 to (CountItem - 1) do
      begin
        with Items.Add do
         begin
            Caption := NGrid[0,i];

            for j := 1 to NGrid.ColCount-1 do
            begin
              SubItems.Add(NGrid[J,i]);
            end;
         end;
      end;
      SortList.CustomSort(@ByFirst, 0);
      NGrid.BeginUpdate;
      for I := 0 to (CountItem - 1) do
      begin
        with Sortlist do
        begin
          NGrid[0,I] := Items[I].Caption;

          for j := 1 to NGrid.ColCount-1 do
          begin
            NGrid[J,I] := Items[I].SubItems[j-1];
          end;
        end;
      end;
      NGrid.EndUpdate;
  End;
end;


procedure CopyGrid(NGrid1,NGrid2: TNiceGrid);
var
  CountItem, I, J, K, L, ThePosition: integer;
begin
      CountItem := NGrid1.RowCount;
      NGrid2.BeginUpdate;
      NGrid2.RowCount := 0;
      for I := 0 to NGrid1.RowCount-1 do
      begin
          NGrid2.AddRow;
          for j := 0 to NGrid2.ColCount-1 do
          begin
            NGrid2[J,I] := NGrid1[j,i];
          end;
      end;
      NGrid2.EndUpdate;
end;

procedure TfMain.ProcessBtnClick(Sender: TObject);
Var i,j: integer;
    Sum3Up,Sum3Tod,Sum3Dwn,Sum4,Sum4Tod,Sum5,Sum5Tod,SumMoney,SumCom : Real;
    sum2up,sum2Tod,Sum2Mee,Sum2Lft,sum2Rth,Sum2Dwn: Real;
    sum1up,sum1Lft,Sum1Mid,Sum1Rth,sum1Dwn,Sum1DLft,Sum1DRth: Real;
begin
    Pbar.Visible := true;

    if InputGrid.RowCount = 0 then
    begin
      Pbar.Visible := false;
      Exit;
    end;

    setlength(Array5,0,3);
    setlength(Array4,0,3);

    Setlength(Array3,4,1000);
    for i := 0 to 3 do
      for j := 0 to 999 do
        Array3[i,j] := '';

    Setlength(Array2,7,100);
    for i := 0 to 6 do
      for j := 0 to 99 do
        Array2[i,j] := '';

    Setlength(Array1,8,10);
    for i := 0 to 7 do
      for j := 0 to 9 do
        Array1[i,j] := '';

    for i := InputGrid.RowCount-1 Downto 0 do
      Translate1(InputGrid[0,i],InputGrid[1,i],InputGrid[2,i],InputGrid[3,i]);


    ProgressBar2.Progress := 0;
    ProgressBar2.MinValue := 0;
    ProgressBar2.MaxValue := 1110 + length(Array4) + length(Array5);

    NiceGrid1.Clear; NiceGrid2.Clear; NiceGrid3.Clear; NiceGrid4.Clear; NiceGrid5.Clear;
    NiceGrid1.RowCount := 0; NiceGrid2.RowCount := 0; NiceGrid3.RowCount := 0; NiceGrid4.RowCount := 0; NiceGrid5.RowCount := 0;
    ClearSumGrid(SumGrid);

    with NiceGrid5 do
    begin
      Sum5  := 0;
      Sum5Tod := 0;
      BeginUpdate;
      RowCount := 0;
      for i := 0 to length(Array5)-1 do
      begin
        if Array5[i,0] <> '' then
        begin
          NiceGrid5.AddRow;
          NiceGrid5[0,Rowcount-1] := Array5[i,0];
          NiceGrid5[1,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array5[i,1],0));
          NiceGrid5[2,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array5[i,2],0));

          Sum5    := Sum5    + StrTofloatDef(Array5[i,1],0);
          Sum5Tod := Sum5Tod + StrTofloatDef(Array5[i,2],0);

        end;
        ProgressBar2.Progress := ProgressBar2.Progress+1;
      end;
      EndUpdate;
      SortGrid(NiceGrid5);
    //------------------------------- Sum ---------------------------//
     {
      SumGrid[0,18] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum5);
      SumGrid[0,19] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum5Tod);

      SumGrid[1,18] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum5    * StrToFloatDef(SumGrid[2,18],0) / 100);
      SumGrid[1,19] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum5Tod * StrToFloatDef(SumGrid[2,19],0) / 100);
     }
    end;


    with NiceGrid4 do
    begin
      Sum4  := 0;
      Sum4Tod := 0;
      BeginUpdate;
      RowCount := 0;
      for i := 0 to length(Array4)-1 do
      begin
        if Array4[i,0] <> '' then
        begin
          NiceGrid4.AddRow;
          NiceGrid4[0,Rowcount-1] := Array4[i,0];
          NiceGrid4[1,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array4[i,1],0));
          NiceGrid4[2,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array4[i,2],0));

          Sum4    := Sum4    + StrTofloatDef(Array4[i,1],0);
          Sum4Tod := Sum4Tod + StrTofloatDef(Array4[i,2],0);
        end;
        ProgressBar2.Progress := ProgressBar2.Progress+1;
      end;
      EndUpdate;
      SortGrid(NiceGrid4);
    //------------------------------- Sum ---------------------------//
     {
      SumGrid[0,16] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum4);
      SumGrid[0,17] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum4Tod);

      SumGrid[1,16] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum4    * StrToFloatDef(SumGrid[2,16],0) / 100);
      SumGrid[1,17] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum4Tod * StrToFloatDef(SumGrid[2,17],0) / 100);
      }
    end;


    with NiceGrid3 do
    begin
      Sum3Up  := 0;
      Sum3Tod := 0;
      Sum3Dwn := 0;
      BeginUpdate;
      RowCount := 0;
      for i := 0 to 999 do
      begin
        if Array3[0,i] <> '' then
        begin
          NiceGrid3.AddRow;
          NiceGrid3[0,Rowcount-1] := Array3[0,i];
          NiceGrid3[1,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array3[1,i],0));//Array3[1,i];
          NiceGrid3[2,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array3[2,i],0));//Array3[2,i];
          NiceGrid3[3,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array3[3,i],0));//Array3[3,i];

          Sum3Up  := Sum3Up+StrTofloatDef(Array3[1,i],0);
          Sum3Tod := Sum3Tod+StrTofloatDef(Array3[2,i],0);
          Sum3Dwn := Sum3Dwn+StrTofloatDef(Array3[3,i],0);

        end;
        ProgressBar2.Progress := ProgressBar2.Progress+1;
      end;
      EndUpdate;
    //------------------------------- Sum ---------------------------//
      {
      SumGrid[0,13] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum3Up);
      SumGrid[0,14] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum3Tod);
      SumGrid[0,15] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum3Dwn);

      SumGrid[1,13] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum3Up  * StrToFloatDef(SumGrid[2,13],0) / 100);
      SumGrid[1,14] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum3Tod * StrToFloatDef(SumGrid[2,14],0) / 100);
      SumGrid[1,15] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum3Dwn * StrToFloatDef(SumGrid[2,15],0) / 100);
      }
    end;

    with NiceGrid2 do
    begin
      sum2up  := 0;
      sum2Tod := 0;
      Sum2Mee := 0;
      Sum2Lft := 0;
      sum2Rth := 0;
      Sum2Dwn := 0;
      BeginUpdate;
      RowCount := 0;
      for i := 0 to 99 do
      begin
        if Array2[0,i] <> '' then
        begin
          NiceGrid2.AddRow;
          NiceGrid2[0,Rowcount-1] := Array2[0,i];
          NiceGrid2[1,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array2[1,i],0));
          NiceGrid2[2,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array2[2,i],0));
          NiceGrid2[3,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array2[3,i],0));
          NiceGrid2[4,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array2[4,i],0));
          NiceGrid2[5,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array2[5,i],0));
          NiceGrid2[6,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array2[6,i],0));

          sum2up  := sum2up  + StrTofloatDef(Array2[1,i],0);
          sum2Tod := sum2Tod + StrTofloatDef(Array2[2,i],0);
          Sum2Mee := Sum2Mee + StrTofloatDef(Array2[3,i],0);
          Sum2Lft := Sum2Lft + StrTofloatDef(Array2[4,i],0);
          sum2Rth := sum2Rth + StrTofloatDef(Array2[5,i],0);
          Sum2Dwn := Sum2Dwn + StrTofloatDef(Array2[6,i],0);
        end;
        ProgressBar2.Progress := ProgressBar2.Progress+1;
      end;
      EndUpdate;
    //------------------------------- Sum ---------------------------//
     {
      SumGrid[0,7] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Up);
      SumGrid[0,8] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Tod);
      SumGrid[0,9] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Mee);
      SumGrid[0,10]:= formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Lft);
      SumGrid[0,11]:= formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Rth);
      SumGrid[0,12]:= formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Dwn);

      SumGrid[1,7] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Up  * StrToFloat(SumGrid[2,7])  / 100 );
      SumGrid[1,8] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Tod * StrToFloat(SumGrid[2,8])  / 100 );
      SumGrid[1,9] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Mee * StrToFloat(SumGrid[2,9])  / 100 );
      SumGrid[1,10]:= formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Lft * StrToFloat(SumGrid[2,10]) / 100 );
      SumGrid[1,11]:= formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Rth * StrToFloat(SumGrid[2,11]) / 100 );
      SumGrid[1,12]:= formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Dwn * StrToFloat(SumGrid[2,12]) / 100 );
     }
    end;

    with NiceGrid1 do
    begin
      sum1up    := 0;
      sum1Lft   := 0;
      Sum1Mid   := 0;
      Sum1Rth   := 0;
      sum1Dwn   := 0;
      Sum1DLft  := 0;
      Sum1DRth  := 0;
      BeginUpdate;
      RowCount := 0;
      for i := 0 to 9 do
      begin
        if Array1[0,i] <> '' then
        begin
          NiceGrid1.AddRow;
          NiceGrid1[0,Rowcount-1] := Array1[0,i];
          NiceGrid1[1,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array1[1,i],0));
          NiceGrid1[2,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array1[2,i],0));
          NiceGrid1[3,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array1[3,i],0));
          NiceGrid1[4,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array1[4,i],0));
          NiceGrid1[5,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array1[5,i],0));
          NiceGrid1[6,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array1[6,i],0));
          NiceGrid1[7,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrTofloatDef(Array1[7,i],0));

          sum1up    := sum1up   + StrTofloatDef(Array1[1,i],0);
          sum1Lft   := sum1Lft  + StrTofloatDef(Array1[2,i],0);
          Sum1Mid   := sum1Mid  + StrTofloatDef(Array1[3,i],0);
          Sum1Rth   := sum1Rth  + StrTofloatDef(Array1[4,i],0);
          sum1Dwn   := sum1Dwn  + StrTofloatDef(Array1[5,i],0);
          Sum1DLft  := sum1DLft + StrTofloatDef(Array1[6,i],0);
          Sum1DRth  := sum1DRth + StrTofloatDef(Array1[7,i],0);

        end;
        ProgressBar2.Progress := ProgressBar2.Progress+1;
      end;
      EndUpdate;
    //------------------------------- Sum ---------------------------//
     {
      SumGrid[0,0] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1up);
      SumGrid[0,1] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Lft);
      SumGrid[0,2] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Mid);
      SumGrid[0,3] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Rth);
      SumGrid[0,4] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Dwn);
      SumGrid[0,5] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1DLft);
      SumGrid[0,6] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1DRth);

      SumGrid[1,0] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1up   * StrTofloat(SumGrid[2,0]) / 100 );
      SumGrid[1,1] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Lft  * StrTofloat(SumGrid[2,1]) / 100 );
      SumGrid[1,2] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Mid  * StrTofloat(SumGrid[2,2]) / 100 );
      SumGrid[1,3] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Rth  * StrTofloat(SumGrid[2,3]) / 100 );
      SumGrid[1,4] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Dwn  * StrTofloat(SumGrid[2,4]) / 100 );
      SumGrid[1,5] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1DLft * StrTofloat(SumGrid[2,5]) / 100 );
      SumGrid[1,6] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1DRth * StrTofloat(SumGrid[2,6]) / 100 );
     }
    end;
    //SumGrid.EndUpdate;
  {
    SumNGrid(NiceGrid1,0);
    SumNGrid(NiceGrid2,7);
    SumNGrid(NiceGrid3,13);
  }
    ProgressBar2.Progress := 0;
    Pbar.Visible := false;
    SumNGrid(NiceGrid1,SumGrid,0);
    SumNGrid(NiceGrid2,SumGrid,7);
    SumNGrid(NiceGrid3,SumGrid,13);
    SumNGrid(NiceGrid4,SumGrid,16);
    SumNGrid(NiceGrid5,SumGrid,18);
    SumsGrid(SumGrid);

    Setlength(Array3,1,1);
end;
