  SortGrid(NiceGrid1);
  SortGrid(NiceGrid2);
  SortGrid(NiceGrid3);
  SortGrid(NiceGrid4);
  SortGrid(NiceGrid5);
end;

procedure TfMain.ComboLotTypeChange(Sender: TObject);
Var found : Boolean;
begin
  with Dm, ComboLotType Do
  begin
    found := Lotto.Locate('LotName',ComboLotType.Items[ItemIndex],[]);
    if found then
      edLotID.Text := Lotto.fieldByName('ID').AsString;
  end;

 Case MainPageControl.TabIndex of
  0:RefreshBtnClick(Sender);
  1:RdgSellClick(Sender);
  2:begin
      ShowCorNum;
      //Showmessage('dfdlfdf');
    end;
 end;  


  with dm do
  begin
    Limit3.Close;
    Limit3.ParamByName('LNum').Value   := PageControlLimit.TabIndex+1;
    Limit3.ParamByName('aDate').Value  := DateToStr(DatePick.Date);
    Limit3.ParamByName('aLotID').Value := StrToInt(edLotID.Text);
    Limit3.Open;
  end;
 
end;

procedure TfMain.PeopleBtnMouseUp(Sender: TObject; Button: TMouseButton;
  Shift: TShiftState; X, Y: Integer);
var P: TPoint;  
begin
  P := PeopleBtn.ClientToScreen(Point(0,PeopleBtn.Height));
  PeopleMenu.Popup(P.x,P.y);
end;

procedure TfMain.N3Click(Sender: TObject);
begin
  With frmCustList do
  begin
    if Showmodal = mrOk then
      FindCustCom(edCustNo.Text)
    else
      FindCustCom(edCustNo.Text);

    SumNGrid(NiceGrid4,SumGrid,16);
    SumNGrid(NiceGrid5,SumGrid,18);
    SumsGrid(SumGrid);
  end
end;

function TfMain.FindCustCom(CustID:String):Boolean;
Var QrFindCust: TABSQuery;
    i: Integer;
begin
  result := false;
  With Dm do
  begin
    QrFindCust := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
    QrFindCust.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
    QrFindCust.Close;
    QrFindCust.SQL.Clear;
    QrFindCust.SQL.Add('Select * from Cust Where CustID = "'+CustID+'"');
    QrFindCust.Open;
    if QrFindCust.RecordCount = 0 then
    begin
      Showmessage('ไม่พบลูกค้าที่ต้องการ กรุณากรอกรหัสลูกให้ถูกต้อง');
      PanelCustName.Caption := '';
      if MainPageControl.TabIndex = 0 then
      begin
        EdCustNo.SetFocus;
        edCustNo.SelectAll;
      end;
      result := false;
    end
    else
    begin
      if MainPageControl.TabIndex = 0 then
        if Isup then
          EdNumber.SetFocus
        else
          edDwnNumber.SetFocus;
      PanelCustName.Caption := ' '+QrFindCust.FieldByName('fName').AsString+' '+QrFindCust.FieldByName('lName').AsString;
      edCustNo.SelectAll;
      Result := true;
    end;

    for i := 0 to SumGrid.Rowcount-1 do
      SumGrid[2,i] := '0';

    if QrFindCust.RecordCount > 0 then
    begin
      SumGrid[2,0]   := QrFindCust.FieldByName('ComRnUp').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,1]   := QrFindCust.FieldByName('ComPosUp').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,2]   := QrFindCust.FieldByName('ComPosUp').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,3]   := QrFindCust.FieldByName('ComPosUp').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,4]   := QrFindCust.FieldByName('ComRnDwn').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,5]   := QrFindCust.FieldByName('ComPosDwn').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,6]   := QrFindCust.FieldByName('ComPosDwn').AsString;

      SumGrid[2,7]   := QrFindCust.FieldByName('Com2Up').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,8]   := QrFindCust.FieldByName('Com2Tod').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,9]   := QrFindCust.FieldByName('Com2Mee').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,10]  := QrFindCust.FieldByName('Com2Pos').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,11]  := QrFindCust.FieldByName('Com2Pos').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,12]  := QrFindCust.FieldByName('Com2Dwn').AsString;

      SumGrid[2,13]  := QrFindCust.FieldByName('Com3Up').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,14]  := QrFindCust.FieldByName('Com3Tod').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,15]  := QrFindCust.FieldByName('Com3Dwn').AsString;

      SumGrid[2,16]  := QrFindCust.FieldByName('Com4').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,17]  := QrFindCust.FieldByName('Com4Tod').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,18]  := QrFindCust.FieldByName('Com5').AsString;
      SumGrid[2,19]  := QrFindCust.FieldByName('Com5Tod').AsString;
    end;
    QrFindCust.Free;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.edCustNoButtonClick(Sender: TObject);
begin
  with frmFindCust,Dm do
  begin
    if Showmodal = mrOk then
    begin
      Application.ProcessMessages;
      with CustList do
      begin
        FindCustCom(Items[selected.index].Caption);

        EdCustNo.Text := Items[selected.index].Caption;
        PanelCustName.Caption := ' '+Items[Selected.index].SubItems[0];
        //EdCustNo.SetFocus;
        if isUp then
          EdNumber.SetFocus
        else
          EdDwnNumber.SetFocus;
        RefreshBtnClick(Sender);
      end;

    end;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.BtnBookNoClick(Sender: TObject);
begin
  with frmFindRef,RefList do
  begin
    if Showmodal = mrOk then
      EdRefNo.Text := Items[itemindex].Caption;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.CutProcBtnClick(Sender: TObject);
Var i,j,k: integer;
    Sum3Up,Sum3Tod,Sum3Dwn,Sum4,Sum4Tod,Sum5,Sum5Tod,SumMoney,SumCom : Real;
    sum2up,sum2Tod,Sum2Mee,Sum2Lft,sum2Rth,Sum2Dwn: Real;
    sum1up,sum1Lft,Sum1Mid,Sum1Rth,sum1Dwn,Sum1DLft,Sum1DRth: Real;    
    QrCutDat,QrCuted: TABSQuery;
    CutNum: String;
    found,FoundLmt: Boolean;
    Limited: Array of Boolean;
    Num5,Num4,Num3,Num2,Num1: TNumber;
begin
  CutGrid1.Clear;
  CutGrid2.Clear;
  CutGrid3.Clear;
  CutGrid4.Clear;
  CutGrid5.Clear;

  CutGrid1.RowCount := 0;
  CutGrid2.RowCount := 0;
  CutGrid3.RowCount := 0;
  CutGrid4.RowCount := 0;
  CutGrid5.RowCount := 0;
  ClearSumGrid(SumCutGrid);
  Application.ProcessMessages;
  With Dm do
  begin
    QrCutDat := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
    QrCutDat.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
    QrCutDat.Close;
    QrCutDat.SQL.Clear;
    QrCutDat.SQL.Add('Select * from Data');
    QrCutDat.SQL.Add('Where (LotType = "'+edLotID.Text+'")');
    QrCutDat.SQL.Add('and (Period_Date ="'+DateToStr(Datepick.Date)+'")');
    QrCutDat.Open;

    if QrCutDat.RecordCount = 0 then
    begin
      QrCutDat.Free;
      CutGrid1.RowCount := 0;
      CutGrid2.RowCount := 0;
      CutGrid3.RowCount := 0;
      CutGrid4.RowCount := 0;
      CutGrid5.RowCount := 0;
      ClearSumGrid(SumCutGrid);
      Pbar.Visible := false;
      Exit;
    end
    else
    begin
      Pbar.Visible := true;
      Application.ProcessMessages;
      Setlength(ArInput,4,QrCutDat.RecordCount);
      for i := 0 to 3 do
        for j := 0 to QrCutDat.RecordCount-1 do
          ArInput[i,j] := '';

      QrCutDat.First;
      for i := 0 to QrCutDat.RecordCount-1 do
      begin
        ArInput[0,i] := QrCutDat.fieldByName('Num').AsString;
        ArInput[1,i] := QrCutDat.fieldByName('Up').AsString;
        ArInput[2,i] := QrCutDat.fieldByName('Dwn').AsString;
        ArInput[3,i] := QrCutDat.fieldByName('ID').AsString;
        QrCutDat.Next;
      end;
    end;

    if RdgSell.ItemIndex > 0 then
    begin
      QrCuted := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
      QrCuted.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
      QrCuted.Close;
      QrCuted.SQL.Clear;
      QrCuted.SQL.Add('Select Num,Sum(Num5) N5,Sum(Num5Tod) N5T,Sum(Num4) N4,Sum(Num4Tod) N4T, Sum(Num3Up) N3Up,Sum(Num3Tod) N3Tod,sum(Num3Dwn) N3Dwn, '+
                      'Sum(Num2Up) N2U,Sum(Num2Tod) N2T,Sum(Num2mee) N2Me,Sum(Num2Left) N2L,Sum(Num2Right) N2R,Sum(Num2Down) N2D, '+
                      'Sum(RunUp) RU,Sum(NumPos1) PU1,Sum(NumPos2) PU2,Sum(NumPos3) PU3,Sum(RunDown) RD,Sum(DownPos1) PD1,Sum(DownPos2) PD2 from Cut');
      QrCuted.SQL.Add('Where (LottoType = "'+edLotID.Text+'")');
      QrCuted.SQL.Add('and (DateCut = "'+DateToStr(Datepick.Date)+'")');
      if (RdgSell.ItemIndex = 3)and(Rdb2.Checked) then
      begin
        QrCuted.SQL.Add('and (ToString(CutDate,"DD/MM/YYYY hh24:nn:ss") = "'+CbCutList.Items[CbCutList.itemindex]+'")');
      end;
      QrCuted.SQL.Add('Group By Num');
      QrCuted.Open;
    end;
  end;


    setlength(ArCut5,0,3);
    setlength(ArCut4,0,3);

    Setlength(ArCut3,4,1000);
    for i := 0 to 3 do
      for j := 0 to 999 do
        ArCut3[i,j] := '';

    Setlength(ArCut2,7,100);
    for i := 0 to 6 do
      for j := 0 to 99 do
        ArCut2[i,j] := '';

    Setlength(ArCut1,8,10);
    for i := 0 to 7 do
      for j := 0 to 9 do
        ArCut1[i,j] := '';


    TabCut.Enabled := false;
    QrCutDat.Last;

    if RdgSell.ItemIndex <> 3 then
    begin
      for i := QrCutDat.RecordCount-1 Downto 0 do
      begin
        TranslateCut(ArInput[0,i], ArInput[1,i], ArInput[2,i], ArInput[3,i]);
      end;
    end;

    QrCutDat.Free;

    ProgressBar2.Progress := 0;
    ProgressBar2.MinValue := 0;
    ProgressBar2.MaxValue := 1110 + length(ArCut4) + length(ArCut5);
    
    Setlength(ArInput,1,1);

    CutGrid1.RowCount := 0;
    CutGrid2.RowCount := 0;
    CutGrid3.RowCount := 0;
    CutGrid4.RowCount := 0;
    CutGrid5.RowCount := 0;
    SumCutGrid.BeginUpdate;

    with CutGrid5,Dm do
    begin
      Sum5  := 0;
      Sum5Tod := 0;
      BeginUpdate;
      RowCount := 0;

      if RdgSell.ItemIndex < 3 then
      for i := 0 to length(ArCut5)-1 do
      begin
        Num5.Num     := ArCut5[i,0];
        Num5.Pr1     := StrToFloatDef(ArCut5[i,1],0);
        Num5.Pr2     := StrToFloatDef(ArCut5[i,2],0);

       if RdgSell.ItemIndex > 0 then
       begin
        Num5.Cuted   := QrCuted.Locate('Num',ArCut5[i,0],[]);
        if Num5.Cuted then
        begin
          Num5.CPr1 := QrCuted.fieldByName('N5').AsFloat;
          Num5.CPr2 := QrCuted.fieldByName('N5T').AsFloat;
        end
        else
        begin
          Num5.CPr1 := 0;
          Num5.CPr2 := 0;
        end;
       end;

       if (RdgSell.ItemIndex = 1)or(RdgSell.ItemIndex = 2) then
       begin
        foundLmt  := TbLocateNum.Locate('LimitDate; LotType; Num',VarArrayOf([DateToStr(DatePick.Date),StrToInt(edLotID.Text),Num5.Num]),[]);
        if found then
        begin
          Num5.Lmt1 := TbLocateNumLimit1.Value;
          Num5.Lmt2 := TbLocateNumLimit2.Value;
          Num5.LPr1 := TbLocateNumPrice1.Value;
          Num5.LPr2 := TbLocateNumPrice2.Value;
        end
        else
        begin
          Num5.Lmt1 := false;
          Num5.Lmt2 := false;
          Num5.LPr1:= 0;
          Num4.LPr2:= 0;
        end;
       end;


        if ArCut5[i,0] <> '' then
        begin
          Case RdgSell.ItemIndex of
            0:begin
                with Num5 do
                begin
                  CutGrid5.AddRow;
                  CutGrid5[0,Rowcount-1] := Num;//ArCut5[i,0];
                  CutGrid5[1,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',Pr1);
                  CutGrid5[2,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',Pr2);

                  Sum5    := Sum5    + Pr1;
                  Sum5Tod := Sum5Tod + Pr2;
                end;
              end;
            1:begin
                found := QrCuted.Locate('Num',ArCut5[i,0],[]);

                if found then
                begin
                    CutGrid5.AddRow;

                    CutGrid5[0,Rowcount-1] := ArCut5[i,0];
                    CutGrid5[1,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut5[i,1],0)-QrCuted.fieldByName('N5').AsFloat);
                    CutGrid5[2,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut5[i,2],0)-QrCuted.fieldByName('N5T').AsFloat);

                    Sum5    := Sum5    + TxtToFloat(CutGrid5[1,Rowcount-1]);
                    Sum5Tod := Sum5Tod + TxtToFloat(CutGrid5[2,Rowcount-1]);
                end
                else
                begin
                    CutGrid5.AddRow;

                    CutGrid5[0,Rowcount-1] := ArCut5[i,0];
                    CutGrid5[1,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut5[i,1],0));
                    CutGrid5[2,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut5[i,2],0));

                    Sum5    := Sum5    + TxtToFloat(CutGrid5[1,Rowcount-1]);
                    Sum5Tod := Sum5Tod + TxtToFloat(CutGrid5[2,Rowcount-1]);
                end;
              end;
            2:begin
                with Num5 do
                begin
                  if foundLmt then
                  begin

                      if Lmt1 then
                      begin
                        if ((Pr1-LPr1-CPr1) > 0) then
                        begin
                          CutGrid5.AddRow;
                          CutGrid5[0,Rowcount-1] := Num;
                          CutGrid5[1,Rowcount-1] := formatfloat('###,###0.##', Pr1-LPr1-CPr1);
                        end;
                      end
                      else
                      if ((Pr1-L5-CPr1) > 0) then
                      begin
                          CutGrid5.AddRow;
                          CutGrid5[0,Rowcount-1] := Num;
                          CutGrid5[1,Rowcount-1] := formatfloat('###,###0.##', Pr1-L5-CPr1);
                      end;

                      if Lmt2 then
                      begin
                        if ((Pr2-LPr2-CPr2) > 0) then
                        begin
                          CutGrid5.AddRow;
                          CutGrid5[0,Rowcount-1] := Num;
                          CutGrid5[2,Rowcount-1] := formatfloat('###,###0.##', Pr2-LPr2-CPr2);
                        end;
                      end
                      else
                      if ((Pr2-L5T-CPr2) > 0) then
                      begin
                          CutGrid5.AddRow;
                          CutGrid5[0,Rowcount-1] := Num;                      
                          CutGrid5[2,Rowcount-1] := formatfloat('###,###0.##', Pr2-L5T-CPr2);
                      end;
                  end
                  else
                  begin
                    if ((Pr1-L5-CPr1) > 0)or((Pr2-L5T-CPr2) > 0) then
                    begin
                      CutGrid5.AddRow;
                      CutGrid5[0,Rowcount-1] := Num;
                      if ((Pr1-L5-CPr1) > 0) then
                        CutGrid5[1,Rowcount-1] := formatfloat('###,###0.##', Pr1-L5-CPr1);
                      if ((Pr2-L5T-CPr2) > 0) then
                        CutGrid5[2,Rowcount-1] := formatfloat('###,###0.##', Pr2-L5T-CPr2);
                    end;
                  end;
                end;
              end;
          end;
        end;
        ProgressBar2.Progress := ProgressBar2.Progress +1;
      end;
      EndUpdate;
      SortGrid(CutGrid5);
    //------------------------------- Sum ---------------------------//

                          CutGrid2[1,Rowcount-1] := formatfloat('###,####.##', Pr1-L2U-CPr1);
                      end;

                      if Lmt2 then

                        begin
                          CutGrid2.AddRow;

                          CutGrid2[2,Rowcount-1] := formatfloat('###,####.##', Pr2-LPr2-CPr2);
                        end;
                      end
                      else
                      if ((Pr2-L2T-CPr2) > 0) then
                      begin
                          CutGrid2.AddRow;
                          CutGrid2[0,Rowcount-1] := Num;
                          CutGrid2[2,Rowcount-1] := formatfloat('###,####.##', Pr2-L2T-CPr2);

                      end

                        CutGrid2[4,Rowcount-1] := formatfloat('###,###0.##', Pr4-L2P-CPr4);
                      if ((Pr5-L2P-CPr5) > 0) then
                        CutGrid2[5,Rowcount-1] := formatfloat('###,###0.##', Pr5-L2P-CPr5);
                      if ((Pr6-L2D-CPr6) > 0) then
                        CutGrid2[6,Rowcount-1] := formatfloat('###,###0.##', Pr6-L2D-CPr6);
                    end;
                  end;
                end;
              end;

               {

          end;

      end;
      EndUpdate;
    //------------------------------- Sum ---------------------------//
      SumCutGrid[0,7]  := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Up);

                begin
                  if ((StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[1,i],0) - LRU - QrCuted.fieldByName('RU').AsFloat)  > 0) or
                     ((StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[2,i],0) - LPU - QrCuted.fieldByName('PU1').AsFloat) > 0) or
                     ((StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[3,i],0) - LPU - QrCuted.fieldByName('PU2').AsFloat) > 0) or
                     ((StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[4,i],0) - LPU - QrCuted.fieldByName('PU3').AsFloat) > 0) or
                     ((StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[5,i],0) - LRD - QrCuted.fieldByName('RD').AsFloat)  > 0) or
                     ((StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[6,i],0) - LPD - QrCuted.fieldByName('PD1').AsFloat) > 0) or
                     ((StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[7,i],0) - LPD - QrCuted.fieldByName('PD2').AsFloat) > 0) then
                  begin
                    CutGrid1.AddRow;

                    CutGrid1[0,Rowcount-1] := ArCut1[0,i];
                    if StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[1,i],0) > (LRU+QrCuted.fieldByName('RU').AsFloat) then
                      CutGrid1[1,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[1,i],0)-LRU-QrCuted.fieldByName('RU').AsFloat);
                    if StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[2,i],0) > LPU then
                      CutGrid1[2,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[2,i],0)-LPU-QrCuted.fieldByName('PU1').AsFloat);
                    if StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[3,i],0) > LPU then
                      CutGrid1[3,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[3,i],0)-LPU-QrCuted.fieldByName('PU2').AsFloat);
                    if StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[4,i],0) > LPU then
                      CutGrid1[4,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[4,i],0)-LPU-QrCuted.fieldByName('PU3').AsFloat);
                    if StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[5,i],0) > LRD then
                      CutGrid1[5,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[5,i],0)-LRD-QrCuted.fieldByName('RD').AsFloat);
                    if StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[6,i],0) > LPD then
                      CutGrid1[6,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[6,i],0)-LPD-QrCuted.fieldByName('PD1').AsFloat);
                    if StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[7,i],0) > LPD then
                      CutGrid1[7,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[7,i],0)-LPD-QrCuted.fieldByName('PD2').AsFloat);

                    sum1up    := sum1up   + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[1,Rowcount-1]);
                    sum1Lft   := sum1Lft  + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[2,Rowcount-1]);
                    sum1Mid   := sum1Mid  + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[3,Rowcount-1]);
                    Sum1Rth   := sum1Rth  + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[4,Rowcount-1]);
                    sum1Dwn   := sum1Dwn  + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[5,Rowcount-1]);
                    sum1DLft  := sum1DLft + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[6,Rowcount-1]);
                    sum1DRth  := sum1DRth + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[7,Rowcount-1])
                  end;
                end
                else
                begin
                  if ((StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[1,i],0) - LRU)  > 0) or
                     ((StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[2,i],0) - LPU)  > 0) or
                     ((StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[3,i],0) - LPU)  > 0) or
                     ((StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[4,i],0) - LPU)  > 0) or
                     ((StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[5,i],0) - LRD)  > 0) or
                     ((StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[6,i],0) - LPD)  > 0) or
                     ((StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[7,i],0) - LPD)  > 0) then
                  begin
                    CutGrid1.AddRow;

                    CutGrid1[0,Rowcount-1] := ArCut1[0,i];
                    if StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[1,i],0) > LRU then
                      CutGrid1[1,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[1,i],0)-LRU);
                    if StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[2,i],0) > LPU then
                      CutGrid1[2,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[2,i],0)-LPU);
                    if StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[3,i],0) > LPU then
                      CutGrid1[3,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[3,i],0)-LPU);
                    if StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[4,i],0) > LPU then
                      CutGrid1[4,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[4,i],0)-LPU);
                    if StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[5,i],0) > LRD then
                      CutGrid1[5,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[5,i],0)-LRD);
                    if StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[6,i],0) > LPD then
                      CutGrid1[6,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[6,i],0)-LPD);
                    if StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[7,i],0) > LPD then
                      CutGrid1[7,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',StrToFloatDef(ArCut1[7,i],0)-LPD);

                    sum1up    := sum1up   + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[1,Rowcount-1]);
                    sum1Lft   := sum1Lft  + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[2,Rowcount-1]);
                    sum1Mid   := sum1Mid  + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[3,Rowcount-1]);
                    Sum1Rth   := sum1Rth  + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[4,Rowcount-1]);
                    sum1Dwn   := sum1Dwn  + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[5,Rowcount-1]);
                    sum1DLft  := sum1DLft + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[6,Rowcount-1]);
                    sum1DRth  := sum1DRth + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[7,Rowcount-1])
                  end;
                end;
             }


              end;
            3:begin
               {
                found := QrCuted.Locate('Num',ArCut1[0,i],[]);

                if found then
                begin
                  CutGrid1.AddRow;

                  CutGrid1[0,Rowcount-1] := QrCuted.fieldByName('Num').AsString;
                  CutGrid1[1,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('RU').AsFloat);
                  CutGrid1[2,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('PU1').AsFloat);
                  CutGrid1[3,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('PU2').AsFloat);
                  CutGrid1[4,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('PU3').AsFloat);
                  CutGrid1[5,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('RD').AsFloat);
                  CutGrid1[6,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('PD1').AsFloat);
                  CutGrid1[7,Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('PD2').AsFloat);

                  sum1up    := sum1up   + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[1,Rowcount-1]);
                  sum1Lft   := sum1Lft  + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[2,Rowcount-1]);
                  sum1Mid   := sum1Mid  + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[3,Rowcount-1]);
                  Sum1Rth   := sum1Rth  + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[4,Rowcount-1]);
                  sum1Dwn   := sum1Dwn  + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[5,Rowcount-1]);
                  sum1DLft  := sum1DLft + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[6,Rowcount-1]);
                  sum1DRth  := sum1DRth + TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[7,Rowcount-1])
                end;
               }
              end;
          end;

        end;
        ProgressBar2.Progress := ProgressBar2.Progress +1;
      end;
      EndUpdate;
    //------------------------------- Sum ---------------------------//
      SumCutGrid[0,0] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1up);
      SumCutGrid[0,1] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Lft);
      SumCutGrid[0,2] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Mid);
      SumCutGrid[0,3] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Rth);
      SumCutGrid[0,4] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Dwn);
      SumCutGrid[0,5] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1DLft);
      SumCutGrid[0,6] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1DRth);
    end;

   //-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------//


    if RdgSell.ItemIndex = 3 then
    begin
      CutGrid5.BeginUpdate;
      CutGrid4.BeginUpdate;
      CutGrid3.BeginUpdate;
      CutGrid2.BeginUpdate;
      CutGrid1.BeginUpdate;
      Pbar.Visible := true;
      ProgressBar2.Progress := 0;
      ProgressBar2.MinValue := 0;
      ProgressBar2.MaxValue := QrCuted.RecordCount;


        QrCuted.First;
        for j := 0 to QrCuted.RecordCount-1 do
        begin

          if length(QrCuted.fieldByName('Num').AsString) = 5 then
          begin
            CutGrid5.AddRow;
            CutGrid5[0,CutGrid5.Rowcount-1] := QrCuted.fieldByName('Num').AsString;
            CutGrid5[1,CutGrid5.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('N5').AsFloat);
            CutGrid5[2,CutGrid5.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('N5T').AsFloat);

            Sum5    := Sum5    + QrCuted.fieldByName('N5').AsFloat;
            Sum5Tod := Sum5Tod + QrCuted.fieldByName('N5T').AsFloat;
          end;

          if length(QrCuted.fieldByName('Num').AsString) = 4 then
          begin
            CutGrid4.AddRow;
            CutGrid4[0,CutGrid4.Rowcount-1] := QrCuted.fieldByName('Num').AsString;
            CutGrid4[1,CutGrid4.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('N4').AsFloat);
            CutGrid4[2,CutGrid4.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('N4T').AsFloat);

            Sum4    := Sum4    + QrCuted.fieldByName('N4').AsFloat;
            Sum4Tod := Sum4Tod + QrCuted.fieldByName('N4T').AsFloat;
          end;

          if length(QrCuted.fieldByName('Num').AsString) = 3 then
          begin
            CutGrid3.AddRow;
            CutGrid3[0,CutGrid3.Rowcount-1] := QrCuted.fieldByName('Num').AsString;
            CutGrid3[1,CutGrid3.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('N3Up').AsFloat);
            CutGrid3[2,CutGrid3.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('N3Tod').AsFloat);
            CutGrid3[3,CutGrid3.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('N3Dwn').AsFloat);

            Sum3Up  := Sum3Up  + QrCuted.fieldByName('N3Up').AsFloat;
            Sum3Tod := Sum3Tod + QrCuted.fieldByName('N3Tod').AsFloat;
            Sum3Dwn := Sum3Dwn + QrCuted.fieldByName('N3Dwn').AsFloat;
          end;

          if length(QrCuted.fieldByName('Num').AsString) = 2 then
          begin
            CutGrid2.AddRow;
            CutGrid2[0,CutGrid2.Rowcount-1] := QrCuted.fieldByName('Num').AsString;
            CutGrid2[1,CutGrid2.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('N2U').AsFloat);
            CutGrid2[2,CutGrid2.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('N2T').AsFloat);
            CutGrid2[3,CutGrid2.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('N2Me').AsFloat);
            CutGrid2[4,CutGrid2.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('N2L').AsFloat);
            CutGrid2[5,CutGrid2.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('N2R').AsFloat);
            CutGrid2[6,CutGrid2.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('N2D').AsFloat);

            sum2up  := sum2up  + QrCuted.fieldByName('N2U').AsFloat;
            sum2Tod := sum2Tod + QrCuted.fieldByName('N2T').AsFloat;
            Sum2Mee := Sum2Mee + QrCuted.fieldByName('N2Me').AsFloat;
            sum2Lft := Sum2Lft + QrCuted.fieldByName('N2L').AsFloat;
            sum2Rth := sum2Rth + QrCuted.fieldByName('N2R').AsFloat;
            sum2Dwn := Sum2Dwn + QrCuted.fieldByName('N2D').AsFloat;
          end;

          if length(QrCuted.fieldByName('Num').AsString) = 1 then
          begin
            CutGrid1.AddRow;
            CutGrid1[0,CutGrid1.Rowcount-1] := QrCuted.fieldByName('Num').AsString;
            CutGrid1[1,CutGrid1.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('RU').AsFloat);
            CutGrid1[2,CutGrid1.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('PU1').AsFloat);
            CutGrid1[3,CutGrid1.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('PU2').AsFloat);
            CutGrid1[4,CutGrid1.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('PU3').AsFloat);
            CutGrid1[5,CutGrid1.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('RD').AsFloat);
            CutGrid1[6,CutGrid1.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('PD1').AsFloat);
            CutGrid1[7,CutGrid1.Rowcount-1] := FormatFloat('###,###;#',QrCuted.fieldByName('PD2').AsFloat);

            sum1up    := sum1up   + QrCuted.fieldByName('RU').AsFloat;
            sum1Lft   := sum1Lft  + QrCuted.fieldByName('PU1').AsFloat;
            sum1Mid   := sum1Mid  + QrCuted.fieldByName('PU2').AsFloat;
            Sum1Rth   := sum1Rth  + QrCuted.fieldByName('PU3').AsFloat;
            sum1Dwn   := sum1Dwn  + QrCuted.fieldByName('RD').AsFloat;
            sum1DLft  := sum1DLft + QrCuted.fieldByName('PD1').AsFloat;
            sum1DRth  := sum1DRth + QrCuted.fieldByName('PD2').AsFloat;
          end;


          ProgressBar2.Progress := ProgressBar2.Progress +1;
          QrCuted.Next;
        end;
        SumCutGrid[0,0] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1up);
        SumCutGrid[0,1] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Lft);
        SumCutGrid[0,2] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Mid);
        SumCutGrid[0,3] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Rth);
        SumCutGrid[0,4] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1Dwn);
        SumCutGrid[0,5] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1DLft);
        SumCutGrid[0,6] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum1DRth);

        SumCutGrid[0,7]  := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Up);
        SumCutGrid[0,8]  := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Tod);
        SumCutGrid[0,9]  := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Mee);
        SumCutGrid[0,10] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Lft);
        SumCutGrid[0,11] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Rth);
        SumCutGrid[0,12] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum2Dwn);

        SumCutGrid[0,13] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum3Up);
        SumCutGrid[0,14] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum3Tod);
        SumCutGrid[0,15] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum3Dwn);

        SumCutGrid[0,16] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum4);
        SumCutGrid[0,17] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum4Tod);

        SumCutGrid[0,18] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum5);
        SumCutGrid[0,19] := formatfloat('#,##0.##;;0',Sum5Tod);

    end;
    CutGrid5.EndUpdate;
    CutGrid4.EndUpdate;
    CutGrid3.EndUpdate;
    CutGrid2.EndUpdate;
    CutGrid1.EndUpdate;

    SumCutGrid.EndUpdate;

    SumNGrid(CutGrid1,SumCutGrid,0);
    SumNGrid(CutGrid2,SumCutGrid,7);
    SumNGrid(CutGrid3,SumCutGrid,13);
    SumNGrid(CutGrid4,SumCutGrid,16);
    SumNGrid(CutGrid5,SumCutGrid,18);

    SumsGrid(SumCutGrid);
    ProgressBar2.Progress := 0;
    Pbar.Visible := false;
    TabCut.Enabled := true;
    
end;

procedure TfMain.BtnCutClick(Sender: TObject);
Var TCutDat: TABSTable;
    i: integer;
    years, months, dates : Word;
    CutTime: TDateTime;
begin
  if findDealer(EditDealer.Text) then
  begin
    //Application.ProcessMessages;
    With Dm do
    begin
      DecodeDate(DatePick.Date,years,months,dates);
      TCutDat := TABSTable.Create(nil);
      TCutDat.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
      TCutDat.TableName := 'Cut';
      TCutDat.Open;

      Pbar.Visible := true;
      ProgressBar2.Progress := 0;
      ProgressBar2.MinValue := 0;
      ProgressBar2.MaxValue := CutGrid5.RowCount + CutGrid4.RowCount + CutGrid3.RowCount + CutGrid2.RowCount + CutGrid1.RowCount;
      CutTime := Now;
      With CutGrid5,TCutDat do
      begin
        for i := 0 to RowCount-1 do
        begin
          Append;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('CutDate').AsDateTime  := CutTime;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('DateCut').AsDateTime  := DatePick.Date;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('LottoType').AsInteger := StrtoInt(edLotID.Text);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num').AsString        := CutGrid5[0,i];
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num5').AsFloat        := TxtToFloat(CutGrid5[1,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num5Tod').AsFloat     := TxtToFloat(CutGrid5[2,i]);
          ProgressBar2.Progress := ProgressBar2.Progress +1;
        end;
      end;

      With CutGrid4,TCutDat do
      begin
        for i := 0 to RowCount-1 do
        begin
          Append;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('CutDate').AsDateTime  := CutTime;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('DateCut').AsDateTime  := DatePick.Date;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('LottoType').AsInteger := StrtoInt(edLotID.Text);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num').AsString        := CutGrid4[0,i];
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num4').AsFloat        := TxtToFloat(CutGrid4[1,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num4Tod').AsFloat     := TxtToFloat(CutGrid4[2,i]);
          ProgressBar2.Progress := ProgressBar2.Progress +1;
        end;
      end;

      With CutGrid3,TCutDat do
      begin
        for i := 0 to RowCount-1 do
        begin
          Append;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('CutDate').AsDateTime  := CutTime;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('DateCut').AsDateTime  := DatePick.Date;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('LottoType').AsInteger := StrtoInt(edLotID.Text);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num').AsString        := CutGrid3[0,i];
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num3Up').AsFloat      := TxtToFloat(CutGrid3[1,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num3Tod').AsFloat     := TxtToFloat(CutGrid3[2,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num3Dwn').AsFloat     := TxtToFloat(CutGrid3[3,i]);
          ProgressBar2.Progress := ProgressBar2.Progress +1;
        end;
      end;

      With CutGrid2,TCutDat do
      begin
        for i := 0 to RowCount-1 do
        begin
          Append;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('CutDate').AsDateTime := CutTime;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('DateCut').AsDateTime := DatePick.Date;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('LottoType').AsInteger:= StrtoInt(edLotID.Text);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num').AsString       := CutGrid2[0,i];
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num2Up').AsFloat     := TxtToFloat(CutGrid2[1,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num2Tod').AsFloat    := TxtToFloat(CutGrid2[2,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num2mee').AsFloat    := TxtToFloat(CutGrid2[3,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num2Left').AsFloat   := TxtToFloat(CutGrid2[4,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num2Right').AsFloat  := TxtToFloat(CutGrid2[5,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num2Down').AsFloat   := TxtToFloat(CutGrid2[6,i]);
          ProgressBar2.Progress := ProgressBar2.Progress +1;
        end;
      end;

      With CutGrid1,TCutDat do
      begin
        for i := 0 to RowCount-1 do
        begin
          Append;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('CutDate').AsDateTime  := CutTime;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('DateCut').AsDateTime  := DatePick.Date;
          TcutDat.FieldByName('LottoType').AsInteger := StrtoInt(edLotID.Text);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('Num').AsString        := CutGrid1[0,i];
          TcutDat.FieldByName('RunUp').AsFloat       := TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[1,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('NumPos1').AsFloat     := TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[2,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('NumPos2').AsFloat     := TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[3,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('NumPos3').AsFloat     := TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[4,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('RunDown').AsFloat     := TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[5,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('DownPos1').AsFloat    := TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[6,i]);
          TcutDat.FieldByName('DownPos2').AsFloat    := TxtToFloat(CutGrid1[7,i]);

          ProgressBar2.Progress := ProgressBar2.Progress +1;
        end;
        Post;
      end;
      TcutDat.Free;
      ProgressBar2.Progress := 0;
      Pbar.Visible := false;
      ClearSumGrid(SumCutGrid);
      CutGrid1.Clear; CutGrid2.Clear; CutGrid3.Clear; CutGrid4.Clear; CutGrid5.Clear;
      CutGrid1.RowCount := 0; CutGrid2.RowCount := 0; CutGrid3.RowCount := 0; CutGrid4.RowCount := 0; CutGrid5.RowCount := 0;
      //CutProcBtnClick(Sender);
    end;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.BtnBackClick(Sender: TObject);
Var
   QrDel : TABSQuery;
begin
 if MessageDlg('ต้องการคืนเลขที่ตัดส่งเจ้ามือใช่หรือไม่? ',mtConfirmation, [mbYes, mbNo], 0) = mrYes then
 begin
  With Dm do
  begin
   QrDel := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
   QrDel.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
   QrDel.Close;
   QrDel.SQL.Clear;
   QrDel.SQL.Add('Delete from Cut');
   QrDel.SQL.Add('Where (DateCut =:aDate)');
   QrDel.SQL.Add('and (lottoType =:aLottoType)');

   if (RdgSell.ItemIndex = 3)and(Rdb2.Checked) then
   begin
     QrDel.SQL.Add('and (ToString(CutDate,"DD/MM/YYYY hh24:nn:ss") = "'+CbCutList.Items[CbCutList.itemindex]+'")');
   end;

   QrDel.ParamByName('aDate').Value  := DateToStr(DatePick.Date);
   QrDel.ParamByName('aLottoType').Value  := StrToInt(edLotID.Text);

   QrDel.ExecSQL;
   QrDel.Free;
   RdgSellClick(Sender);
   //CutProcBtnClick(Sender);
  end;
 end

end;

begin
  //Case MainPageControl.ActivePageIndex of
    //0: RefreshBtnClick(Sender);
    //1: RdgSellClick(Sender);
  //end;

{
 Case MainPageControl.TabIndex of
  0:RefreshBtnClick(Sender);
  1:RdgSellClick(Sender);
  2:ShowCorNum;
 end;
}

end;

procedure TfMain.DatePickCloseUp(Sender: TObject);
begin
  if DatePick.Date <> MainDate then
    ComboLotTypeChange(Sender);

{
  Application.ProcessMessages;
  Case MainPageControl.ActivePageIndex of
    0: RefreshBtnClick(Sender);
    1: RdgSellClick(Sender);//CutProcBtnClick(Sender);
  end;
 }
end;

procedure TfMain.edRefNoChange(Sender: TObject);
begin

procedure TfMain.FormKeyDown(Sender: TObject; var Key: Word;
  Shift: TShiftState);
begin
  if MainPageControl.ActivePageIndex = 0 then
  begin
    if Key = VK_F4 then
    begin
      edCustNoButtonClick(Sender);
      Key := 0;
    end;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.FindInputBtnClick(Sender: TObject);
begin
  RefreshBtnClick(Sender);
end;

procedure TfMain.edRefNoKeyPress(Sender: TObject; var Key: Char);
begin
  if Key = #13 then
  begin
    edRefNo.SelectAll;
    RefreshBtnClick(Sender);
    Key := #0;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.ChkSortInputChange(Sender: TObject);
Var QrDat: TABSQuery;
begin
  With Dm do
  begin
   if Not Chklook.Checked then
   begin
    QrDat := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
    QrDat.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
    QrDat.Close;
    QrDat.SQL.Clear;
    QrDat.SQL.Add('Select * from Data');
    QrDat.SQL.Add('Where (LotType = "'+edLotID.Text+'")');
    QrDat.SQL.Add('and (Period_Date ="'+DateToStr(Datepick.Date)+'")');
    if Not ChkSumBook.Checked then
      QrDat.SQL.Add('and (RefNo = "'+edRefNo.Text +'")');
    if Not ChkSumCust.Checked then
      QrDat.SQL.Add('and (CustNo = "'+edCustNo.Text+'")');

    if Not ChkSortInput.Checked then
      QrDat.SQL.Add('Order By ID DESC')
    else
      QrDat.SQL.Add('Order By ID ASC');
    QrDat.Open;

    InputGrid.RowCount := 0;
    QrDat.First;
    InputGrid.BeginUpDate;
    While Not QrDat.Eof do
    begin
      InputGrid.AddRow;
      InputGrid[0,InputGrid.RowCount-1] := QrDat.fieldByName('Num').AsString;
      InputGrid[1,InputGrid.RowCount-1] := QrDat.fieldByName('Up').AsString;
      InputGrid[2,InputGrid.RowCount-1] := QrDat.fieldByName('Dwn').AsString;
      InputGrid[3,InputGrid.RowCount-1] := QrDat.fieldByName('ID').AsString;

      QrDat.Next;
    end;
    InputGrid.EndUpDate;
    QrDat.Free;
    lbItems1.Caption := IntToStr(inputGrid.RowCount)+' รายการ';
   end
   else
    Look;
  end;

end;

procedure TfMain.Panel13Resize(Sender: TObject);
begin
  Panel14.Width := (Panel13.Width-4) div 2;
end;

procedure TfMain.FormClose(Sender: TObject; var Action: TCloseAction);
Var IniFile : TInifile;
begin
    //NiceSettings1.SaveSettings;
    IniFile := TIniFile.Create(
             ChangeFileExt(Application.ExeName,'.ini'));

    IniFile.WriteString('DB','DBFile',Dm.Database.DatabaseFileName);
    IniFile.WriteInteger('TAB','TabNum',NumInputPC.TabIndex);
    IniFile.WriteInteger('TAB','TabCut',PageControlCutNum.TabIndex);
    IniFile.WriteInteger('TAB','TabMain',MainPageControl.TabIndex);

    IniFile.Free;
    Application.Terminate;
end;

procedure TfMain.RefreshBtnClick(Sender: TObject);
Var QrDat: TABSQuery;
    i,j: Integer;
begin
  NiceGrid1.Clear;
  NiceGrid2.Clear;
  NiceGrid3.Clear;
  NiceGrid4.Clear;
  NiceGrid5.Clear;
  InputGrid.Clear;

  NiceGrid1.RowCount := 0;
  NiceGrid2.RowCount := 0;
  NiceGrid3.RowCount := 0;
  NiceGrid4.RowCount := 0;
  NiceGrid5.RowCount := 0;
  InputGrid.RowCount := 0;
  ClearSumGrid(SumGrid);
  Pbar.Visible := true;
  Application.ProcessMessages;

  SumGrid.Columns[1].Visible := Not ChkSumCust.Checked;
  With Dm do
  begin
   if Not Chklook.Checked then
   begin
    QrDat := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
    QrDat.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
    QrDat.Close;
    QrDat.SQL.Clear;
    QrDat.SQL.Add('Select * from Data');
    QrDat.SQL.Add('Where (LotType = "'+edLotID.Text+'")');
    QrDat.SQL.Add('and (Period_Date = "'+DateToStr(Datepick.Date)+'")');

    if Not ChkSumBook.Checked then
      QrDat.SQL.Add('and (RefNo = "'+edRefNo.Text +'")');
    if Not ChkSumCust.Checked then
      QrDat.SQL.Add('and (CustNo = "'+edCustNo.Text+'")');


    if Not ChkSortInput.Checked then
      QrDat.SQL.Add('Order By ID DESC')
    else
      QrDat.SQL.Add('Order By ID ASC');
    QrDat.Open;

    InputGrid.BeginUpDate;
    InputGrid.Clear;
    InputGrid.RowCount := 0;

    QrDat.First;
    //for i := 0 to QrDat.RecordCount-1 do
    While Not QrDat.Eof do
    begin
      //Application.ProcessMessages;
      InputGrid.AddRow;
      InputGrid[0,InputGrid.RowCount-1] := QrDat.fieldByName('Num').AsString;
      InputGrid[1,InputGrid.RowCount-1] := QrDat.fieldByName('Up').AsString;
      InputGrid[2,InputGrid.RowCount-1] := QrDat.fieldByName('Dwn').AsString;
      InputGrid[3,InputGrid.RowCount-1] := QrDat.fieldByName('ID').AsString;
      QrDat.Next;
    end;
    QrDat.Free;

    InputGrid.EndUpDate;
    lbItems1.Caption := IntToStr(inputGrid.RowCount)+' รายการ';
    ProcessBtnClick(Sender);
   end
   else
    Look;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.edCustNoKeyPress(Sender: TObject; var Key: Char);
begin
  if Key = #13 then
  begin
    if FindCustCom(edCustNo.Text) then
      RefreshBtnClick(Sender)
    else
    begin
      InputGrid.Clear;
      NiceGrid1.Clear;
      NiceGrid2.Clear;
      NiceGrid3.Clear;
      NiceGrid4.Clear;
      NiceGrid5.Clear;
      
      InputGrid.RowCount := 0;
      NiceGrid1.RowCount := 0;
      NiceGrid2.RowCount := 0;
      NiceGrid3.RowCount := 0;
      NiceGrid4.RowCount := 0;
      NiceGrid5.RowCount := 0;
      ClearSumGrid(SumGrid);
    end;
    Key := #0;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.edCustNoExit(Sender: TObject);
begin
  if MainPageControl.TabIndex = 0 then
    FindCustCom(edCustNo.Text);
end;

procedure TfMain.TabInputShow(Sender: TObject);
begin
  Application.ProcessMessages;
  //RefreshBtnClick(Sender);
  with Dm do
  begin
    TAllData.Close;
    TAllData.Open;
  end;
  
  if isup then
    edNumber.SetFocus
  else
    edDwnNumber.SetFocus;
  
end;

procedure GetLotLimit(LotID:String);
VAr QrLotType: TABSQuery;
begin
  with Dm do
  begin
    QrLotType := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
    QrLotType.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
    QrLotType.Close;
    QrLotType.SQL.Clear;
    QrlotType.SQL.Add('Select * from Lotto');
    QrlotType.SQL.Add('Where ID = :aID');
    QrlotType.ParamByName('aID').Value := LotID;
    QrlotType.Open;

    QrLotType.First;
    while Not QrLotType.Eof do
    begin
      LRU := QrLotType.fieldByName('LimitRnUp').AsFloat;

      LPU := QrLotType.fieldByName('LimitPos').AsFloat;

      LRD := QrLotType.fieldByName('LimitRnDwn').AsFloat;

      LPD := QrLotType.fieldByName('LimitPosDwn').AsFloat;

      L2U := QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit2Up').AsFloat;

      L2T := QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit2Tod').AsFloat;

      L2Me := QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit2mee').AsFloat;

      L2P := QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit2Pos').AsFloat;

      L2D := QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit2Dwn').AsFloat;

      L3U := QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit3Up').AsFloat;

      L3T := QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit3Tod').AsFloat;

      L3D := QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit3Dwn').AsFloat;

      L4 := QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit4').AsFloat;

      L4T := QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit4Tod').AsFloat;

      L5 := QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit5').AsFloat;

      L5T := QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit5Tod').AsFloat;

      QrLotType.Next;
    end;
    QrLotType.Free;
  end;
end;


procedure TfMain.TabCutShow(Sender: TObject);
begin
  Application.ProcessMessages;

  Application.ProcessMessages;
  GetLotLimit(edLotID.Text);
  Dm.TbLocateNum.Close;
  Dm.TbLocateNum.Open;
  RdgSellClick(Sender);  
end;

procedure TfMain.Open1Click(Sender: TObject);
begin
  With Dm do
  begin
    if OpenDBDialog.Execute then
    begin
     try
      Application.ProcessMessages;
      Database.Connected := false;
      Database.DatabaseFileName := OpenDBDialog.FileName;
      Database.Connected := true;
      QrData.Open;
      Lotto.Open;
      Limit3.Open;
      TbLocateNum.Open;
      TAllData.Open;
      Caption := 'Big LOTTO 2.0 - '+Dm.Database.DatabaseFileName;
      Statusbar.Panels[0].Text := 'Big LOTTO 2.0 (ติดต่อ โทร : 084-0643183) - '+Dm.Database.DatabaseFileName;
     {
      Case MainPageControl.TabIndex of
        0:RefreshBtnClick(Sender);
        1:RdgSellClick(Sender);
        2:ShowCorNum;
      end;
     }
      LogoutBtnClick(Sender);
     except
     end;
    end;

  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.CutPrintBtnClick(Sender: TObject);
begin
   begin
      frmPrintCutPrev.RenderGrids(SumCutGrid,CutGrid1,CutGrid2,CutGrid3,CutGrid4,CutGrid5,DatePick.Date,ComboLotType.Items[ComboLotType.itemindex] ,RdgSell.ItemIndex);
      frmPrintCutPrev.Showmodal;
   end;
end;

procedure TfMain.TabLotTypeShow(Sender: TObject);
VAr QrLotType: TABSQuery;
begin
  Application.ProcessMessages;
  with Dm,LotTypeGrid do
  begin
    QrLotType := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
    QrLotType.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
    QrLotType.Close;
    QrLotType.SQL.Clear;
    QrlotType.SQL.Add('Select * from Lotto Order By ID');
    QrlotType.Open;

    LotTypeGrid.Clear;
    LotTypeGrid.RowCount := 0;
    LotTypeGrid.BeginUpdate;
    QrLotType.First;
    while Not QrLotType.Eof do
    begin
      AddRow;
      LotTypeGrid[0,RowCount-1] := QrLotType.fieldByName('LotName').asString;
      LotTypeGrid[1,RowCount-1] := QrLotType.fieldByName('ID').asString;

      LotTypeGrid[2,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('LimitRnUp').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[3,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('PayRnUp').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[4,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('ComRnUp').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[5,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('LimitPos').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[6,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('PayPos').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[7,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('ComPos').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[8,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('LimitRnDwn').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[9,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('PayRnDwn').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[10,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('ComRnDwn').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[11,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('LimitPosDwn').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[12,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('PayPosDwn').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[13,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('ComPosDwn').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[14,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit2Up').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[15,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Pay2Up').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[16,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Com2Up').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[17,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit2Tod').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[18,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Pay2Tod').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[19,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Com2Tod').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[20,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit2mee').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[21,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Pay2mee').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[22,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Com2mee').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[23,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit2Pos').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[24,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Pay2Pos').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[25,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Com2Pos').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[26,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit2Dwn').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[27,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Pay2Dwn').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[28,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Com2Dwn').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[29,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit3Up').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[30,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Pay3Up').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[31,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Com3Up').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[32,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit3Tod').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[33,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Pay3Tod').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[34,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Com3Tod').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[35,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit3Dwn').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[40,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Com4').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[41,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit4Tod').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[42,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Pay4Tod').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[44,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit5').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[46,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Com5').AsFloat);

      LotTypeGrid[47,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Limit5Tod').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[48,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Pay5Tod').AsFloat);
      LotTypeGrid[49,RowCount-1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',QrLotType.fieldByName('Com5Tod').AsFloat);

      QrLotType.Next;
    end;
    LotTypeGrid.EndUpdate;
    ShowSetting;
    QrLotType.Free;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.ShowSetting;
begin
  with LotTypeGrid do
  begin
    SetGrid[0,0] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',TxtToFloat(LotTypeGrid[2,Row]));
    SetGrid[1,0] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',TxtToFloat(LotTypeGrid[3,Row]));
    SetGrid[2,0] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',TxtToFloat(LotTypeGrid[4,Row]));

    SetGrid[0,1] := FormatFloat('###,##0;0',TxtToFloat(LotTypeGrid[5,Row]));

procedure TfMain.LotTypeGridClick(Sender: TObject);
begin
  ShowSetting;
end;

procedure TfMain.LotTypeGridKeyDown(Sender: TObject; var Key: Word;
  Shift: TShiftState);
begin
  if (Key = VK_Up)or(Key = VK_Down) then
  begin
    ShowSetting;
    Key := 0;
  end;

  if (Key = VK_Delete) then
  begin
    with LotTypeGrid do
      DeleteRow(Row);

    ShowSetting;
    Key := 0;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.SeSkinButton7Click(Sender: TObject);
Var QrUpDateSet: TABSQuery;
    i,j,k: integer;
begin
   with Dm, LotTypeGrid do
   begin
      QrUpDateSet := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
      QrUpDateSet.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
      QrUpDateSet.Close;
      QrUpDateSet.SQL.Clear;
      QrUpDateSet.SQL.Add('Update Lotto');
      QrUpDateSet.SQL.Add('Set LotName=:aLotname, ' +
                           'LimitRnUp=:aLRnU, LimitPos=:aLPU, LimitRnDwn=:aLRnD, LimitPosDwn=:aLPD, Limit2Up=:aL2U, Limit2Tod=:aL2T, Limit2Mee=:aL2M, Limit2Dwn=:aL2D, '+
                           'Limit2Pos=:aL2P, Limit3Up=:aL3U, Limit3Tod=:aL3T, Limit3Dwn=:aL3D, Limit4=:aL4, Limit4Tod=:aL4Tod, Limit5=:aL5, Limit5Tod=:aL5Tod, '+

                           'ComRnUp=:aCRnU, ComPos=:aCPU, ComRnDwn=:aCRnD, ComPosDwn=:aCPD, Com2Up=:aC2U, Com2Tod=:aC2T, Com2Mee=:aC2M, Com2Dwn=:aC2D, '+
                           'Com2Pos=:aC2P, Com3Up=:aC3U, Com3Tod=:aC3T, Com3Dwn=:aC3D, Com4=:aC4, Com4Tod=:aC4Tod, Com5=:aC5, Com5Tod=:aC5Tod, '+

                           'PayRnUp=:aPRnU, PayPos=:aPPU, PayRnDwn=:aPRnD, PayPosDwn=:aPPD, Pay2Up=:aP2U, Pay2Tod=:aP2T, Pay2Mee=:aP2M, Pay2Dwn=:aP2D, '+
                           'Pay2Pos=:aP2P, Pay3Up=:aP3U, Pay3Tod=:aP3T, Pay3Dwn=:aP3D, Pay4=:aP4, Pay4Tod=:aP4Tod, Pay5=:aP5, Pay5Tod=:aP5Tod ');

      QrUpDateSet.SQL.Add('where ID =:aID');

      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aLotName').Value  := LotTypeGrid[0,Row];
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aID').Value  := LotTypeGrid[1,Row];

      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aLRnU').Value := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,0]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aLPU').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,1]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aLRnD').Value := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,2]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aLPD').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,3]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aL2U').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,4]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aL2T').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,5]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aL2M').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,6]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aL2D').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,7]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aL2P').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,8]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aL3U').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,9]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aL3T').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,10]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aL3D').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,11]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aL4').Value   := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,12]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aL4Tod').Value:= TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,13]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aL5').Value   := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,14]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aL5Tod').Value:= TxtToFloat(SetGrid[0,15]);

      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aPPU').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[1,1]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aPRnD').Value := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[1,2]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aPPD').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[1,3]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP2U').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[1,4]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP2T').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[1,5]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP2M').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[1,6]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP2D').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[1,7]);

      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC2U').Value := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[2,4]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC2T').Value := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[2,5]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC2M').Value := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[2,6]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC2D').Value := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[2,7]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC2P').Value := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[2,8]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC3U').Value := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[2,9]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC3T').Value := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[2,10]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC3D').Value := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[2,11]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC4').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[2,12]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC4Tod').Value:= TxtToFloat(SetGrid[2,13]);

      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC5Tod').Value  := TxtToFloat(SetGrid[2,15]);

      QrUpDateSet.ExecSQL;

   try
    if ChkSaveTemp.Checked then // อัพเดท ค่าเริ่มต้น
    begin
      QrUpDateSet.Close;
      QrUpDateSet.SQL.Clear;
      QrUpDateSet.SQL.Add('Update TempLotto');
      QrUpDateSet.SQL.Add('Set ComRnUp=:aCRnU, ComRnDwn=:aCRnD, ComPosUp=:aCPU, ComPosDwn=:aCPD, Com2Up=:aC2U, Com2Tod=:aC2T, Com2Dwn=:aC2D, Com2Mee=:aC2M, '+
                          'Com2Pos=:aC2P, Com3Up=:aC3U, Com3Tod=:aC3T, Com3Dwn=:aC3D, Com4=:aC4, Com4Tod=:aC4Tod, Com5=:aC5, Com5Tod=:aC5Tod, '+
                          'PayRnUp=:aPRnU, PayRnDwn=:aPRnD, PayPosUp=:aPPU, PayPosDwn=:aPPD, Pay2Up=:aP2U, Pay2Tod=:aP2T, Pay2Dwn=:aP2D, Pay2Mee=:aP2M, '+
                          'Pay2Pos=:aP2P, Pay3Up=:aP3U, Pay3Tod=:aP3T, Pay3Dwn=:aP3D, Pay4=:aP4, Pay4Tod=:aP4Tod, Pay5=:aP5, Pay5Tod=:aP5Tod ');

      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aCRnU').Value  := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,0]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aCRnD').Value  := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,1]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aCPU').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,2]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aCPD').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,3]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC2U').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,4]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC2T').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,5]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC2D').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,6]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC2M').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,7]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC2P').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,8]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC3U').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,9]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC3T').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,10]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC3D').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,11]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC4').Value    := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,12]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC4Tod').Value := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,13]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC5').Value    := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,14]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aC5Tod').Value := StrToFloat(SetGrid[2,15]);

      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aPRnU').Value  := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,0]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aPRnD').Value  := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,1]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aPPU').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,2]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aPPD').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,3]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP2U').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,4]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP2T').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,5]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP2D').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,6]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP2M').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,7]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP2P').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,8]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP3U').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,9]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP3T').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,10]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP3D').Value   := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,11]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP4').Value    := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,12]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP4Tod').Value := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,13]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP5').Value    := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,14]);
      QrUpDateSet.ParamByName('aP5Tod').Value := StrToFloat(SetGrid[1,15]);
      QrUpDateSet.ExecSQL;
    end;
   except
    QrUpDateSet.Free;
    Showmessage('ไม่สามารถบันทึกค่าเริ่มต้นได้');
   end;

      j := -1;
      for i := 2 to LotTypeGrid.ColCount-1 do
      begin
        k := ((i-2) mod 3);
        if ((i-2) mod 3) = 0 then
        begin
          j := j+1;
        end;
        LotTypeGrid[i,Row] := SetGrid[k,j];
      end;

      Showmessage('ข้อมูลได้รับการบันทึกแก้ไขเรียบร้อยแล้ว');
      ShowSetting;
   end;  
end;

procedure TfMain.FormResize(Sender: TObject);
begin
    Pbar.Left := (width  - Pbar.Width)  div 2;
    Pbar.Top  := (Height - Pbar.Height) div 2;
    PnInputM.Width := Width-(PnInputL.Width+PnInputR.Width+Toollbar.Width+23);
    PnCutM.Width := Width-(PnCutL.Width+PnCutR.Width+Toollbar.Width+23);
    //statusbar.Panels[0].Width := statusbar.width-ProgressBar1.Width-10;
    statusbar.Panels[0].Width := statusbar.width-50;

end;

end;

procedure TfMain.RdgSellClick(Sender: TObject);
Var QrItemsCut: TABSQuery;
    i: integer;
begin
  GroupBox1.Visible := RdgSell.ItemIndex = 3;
  Application.ProcessMessages;
  if rdgSell.ItemIndex = 3 then
  with Dm do
  begin
    QrItemsCut := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
    QrItemsCut.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
    QrItemsCut.SQL.Add('Select CutDate from Cut');
    QrItemsCut.SQL.Add('Where ((DateCut = :aDate)');
    QrItemsCut.SQL.Add('And (LottoType = :aLotType))');
    QrItemsCut.SQL.Add('Group By CutDate');
    QrItemsCut.SQL.Add('Order By CutDate DESC');
    QrItemsCut.ParamByName('aDate').Value    := DateToStr(DatePick.Date);
    QrItemsCut.ParamByName('aLotType').Value := StrToInt(edLotID.Text);
    QrItemsCut.Open;

    QrItemsCut.First;
    CbCutlist.Clear;
    for i := 0 to QrItemsCut.RecordCount-1 do
    begin
      CbCutList.Items.Add(FormatDateTime('dd/mm/yyyy hh:mm:ss',QrItemsCut.fieldByName('CutDate').AsDateTime));// .AsString);
      QrItemsCut.Next;
    end;
    QrItemsCut.Free;
    CbCutList.ItemIndex := 0;
  end;
  CutProcBtnClick(Sender);
end;

procedure TfMain.CbCutListChange(Sender: TObject);
begin
  if Not Rdb2.Checked then
    Rdb2.Checked := true
  else
    CutProcBtnClick(Sender);
end;

{
procedure TfMain.ReadPrinterConfig;
var
  PrinterDpiWidth, PrinterDpiHeight: Integer;
  PrinterPageWidth, PrinterPageHeight: Integer;
  ScreenDpiWidth, ScreenDpiHeight: Integer;
  Dc: HDC;
begin
  try
    PrinterDpiWidth   := GetDeviceCaps(Printer.Handle, LOGPIXELSX);
    PrinterDpiHeight  := GetDeviceCaps(Printer.Handle, LOGPIXELSY);
    PrinterPageWidth  := GetDeviceCaps(Printer.handle, PHYSICALWIDTH);
    PrinterPageHeight := GetDeviceCaps(Printer.handle, PHYSICALHEIGHT);
  except
    PrinterDpiWidth   := 360;    // Assumption
    PrinterDpiHeight  := 360;    // This happens when no printer installed.
    PrinterPageWidth  := 3060;
    PrinterPageHeight := 3960;
  end;
  Dc := GetDC(0);
  ScreenDpiWidth  := GetDeviceCaps(Dc, LOGPIXELSX);
  ScreenDpiHeight := GetDeviceCaps(Dc, LOGPIXELSY);
  ReleaseDc(0, Dc);
  FPageWidth  := Round((PrinterPageWidth / PrinterDpiWidth) * ScreenDpiWidth);
  FPageHeight := Round((PrinterPageHeight / PrinterDpiHeight) * ScreenDpiHeight);
end;
}

procedure TfMain.PrintCustBtnClick(Sender: TObject);
Var i,y,x,txtHigh,MidPage: integer;
    PriceLen: Array[1..2] of integer;
    LastPage,RmLine : integer;
begin

  with PrintDialog1, InputGrid do
  begin
    //Options := [poPageNums, poSelection,PoHelp];
    FromPage := 1;
    MinPage  := 1;
    if (InputGrid.RowCount Div 60) = 0 then
      RmLine := 0
    else
      RmLine := (InputGrid.RowCount Div 60);


    //showmessage(IntToStr(RmLine));
    //exit;

    if RmLine > 0 then
      LastPage := 1
    else
      LastPage := 0;
    MaxPage  := (InputGrid.RowCount Div 60)+LastPage;
    ToPage   := (InputGrid.RowCount div 60)+LastPage;
    if (SelectArea.Bottom-SelectArea.Top) > 0 then
       PrintRange := prSelection
    else
       PrintRange := prAllPages;

    //Options  := [poPageNums,poSelection,poHelp]; {allow page selection}
    Options  := [poSelection,poHelp]; {allow page selection}



    if execute then
    begin
      Printer.BeginDoc;

      x := 300;
      y := 700;
      MidPage := (Printer.PageWidth div 2)- (Printer.Canvas.TextWidth(ComboLotType.Items[CombolotType.itemindex]) div 2);
      Printer.Canvas.Font.Size := 10;
      Printer.Canvas.TextOut(MidPage,y-500,ComboLotType.Items[CombolotType.itemindex]);
      Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x,y-200,'ลูกค้า : '+edCustNo.Text+' '+PanelcustName.Caption+' เล่มที่ '+edRefNo.text+' วันที่'+'  '+DateToStr(DatePick.Date));
      Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x-100,y-100,'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------');

      if PrintRange = prSelection then
      begin
        for i := SelectArea.Top to SelectArea.Bottom do
        begin
          //Pricelen[1] := length(InputGrid[1,i]);
          //Pricelen[2] := length(InputGrid[2,i]);

          Pricelen[1] := Printer.Canvas.TextWidth(InputGrid[1,i]);
          Pricelen[2] := Printer.Canvas.TextWidth(InputGrid[2,i]);

          Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x,y,IntToStr(i+1));
          Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x+300,y,InputGrid[0,i]);
          Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x+(550)+((800-Pricelen[1])),y,InputGrid[1,i]);
          Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x+(1200)+((800-Pricelen[2])),y,InputGrid[2,i]);

          txtHigh := Printer.Canvas.TextHeight(InputGrid[0,i]);
          Inc(y,txtHigh);

          if (((i+1) mod 60) = 0) then
          begin
            Printer.EndDoc;
            exit;
            y := 700;
            Printer.NewPage;
            Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x,y-200,'ลูกค้า : '+edCustNo.Text+' '+PanelcustName.Caption+' เล่มที่ '+edRefNo.text+' วันที่'+'  '+DateToStr(DatePick.Date));
            Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x-100,y-100,'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------');
          end;

        end;
      end
      else
      if PrintRange = prPageNums then
      begin
        if toPage < maxpage then
        begin
          for i := (((fromPage*59) div 60)*60)  to (((ToPage*59) div 60)*60)+59 do
          begin
            //if i = 1 then

            Pricelen[1] := Printer.Canvas.TextWidth(InputGrid[1,i]);
            Pricelen[2] := Printer.Canvas.TextWidth(InputGrid[2,i]);

            Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x,y,IntToStr(i+1));
            Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x+300,y,InputGrid[0,i]);
            Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x+(550)+((800-Pricelen[1])),y,InputGrid[1,i]);
            Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x+(1200)+((800-Pricelen[2])),y,InputGrid[2,i]);

            txtHigh := Printer.Canvas.TextHeight(InputGrid[0,i]);
            Inc(y,txtHigh);

            if (((i+1) mod 60) = 0) then
            begin
              Printer.EndDoc;
              exit;
              y := 700;
              Printer.NewPage;
              Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x,y-200,'ลูกค้า : '+edCustNo.Text+' '+PanelcustName.Caption+' เล่มที่ '+edRefNo.text+' วันที่'+'  '+DateToStr(DatePick.Date));
              Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x-100,y-100,'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------');

            end;
          end;
        end
        else
        begin
          for i := (((fromPage*59) div 60)*60)  to (((ToPage*59) div 60)*60)+(RmLine-1) do
          begin
            Pricelen[1] := Printer.Canvas.TextWidth(InputGrid[1,i]);
            Pricelen[2] := Printer.Canvas.TextWidth(InputGrid[2,i]);

            Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x,y,IntToStr(i+1));
            Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x+300,y,InputGrid[0,i]);
            Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x+(550)+((800-Pricelen[1])),y,InputGrid[1,i]);
            Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x+(1200)+((800-Pricelen[2])),y,InputGrid[2,i]);

            txtHigh := Printer.Canvas.TextHeight(InputGrid[0,i]);
            Inc(y,txtHigh);

            if (((i+1) mod 60) = 0) then
            begin
              Printer.EndDoc;
              exit;
              y := 700;
              Printer.NewPage;
              Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x,y-200,'ลูกค้า : '+edCustNo.Text+' '+PanelcustName.Caption+' เล่มที่ '+edRefNo.text+' วันที่'+'  '+DateToStr(DatePick.Date));
              Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x-100,y-100,'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------');

            end;
          end;
        end;


      end
      else
      if PrintRange = prAllPages then
      begin
        for i := 0 to Rowcount-1 do
        begin
          Pricelen[1] := Printer.Canvas.TextWidth(InputGrid[1,i]);
          Pricelen[2] := Printer.Canvas.TextWidth(InputGrid[2,i]);

          Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x,y,IntToStr(i+1));
          Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x+300,y,InputGrid[0,i]);
          Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x+(550)+((800-Pricelen[1])),y,InputGrid[1,i]);
          Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x+(1200)+((800-Pricelen[2])),y,InputGrid[2,i]);

          txtHigh := Printer.Canvas.TextHeight(InputGrid[0,i]);
          Inc(y,txtHigh);

          if (((i+1) mod 60) = 0) then
          begin
            Printer.EndDoc;
            exit;
            y := 700;
            Printer.NewPage;
            Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x,y-200,'ลูกค้า : '+edCustNo.Text+' '+PanelcustName.Caption+' เล่มที่ '+edRefNo.text+' วันที่'+'  '+DateToStr(DatePick.Date));
            Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x-100,y-100,'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------');
          end;

        end;
      end;

      Printer.Canvas.TextOut(x-100,y,'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------');
      Printer.EndDoc;
    end
    else
      Exit;
  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.ApplicationEvents1Message(var Msg: tagMSG;
  var Handled: Boolean);
begin
  with RdgSell do
  begin
    BtnCut.Enabled       := (ItemIndex = 2)and((CutGrid1.RowCount > 0)or(CutGrid2.RowCount > 0)or
                            (CutGrid3.RowCount > 0)or(CutGrid4.RowCount > 0)or(CutGrid5.RowCount > 0));
    BtnBack.Enabled      := (ItemIndex = 3)and((CutGrid1.RowCount > 0)or(CutGrid2.RowCount > 0)or
                            (CutGrid3.RowCount > 0)or(CutGrid4.RowCount > 0)or(CutGrid5.RowCount > 0));
    XlsExportBtn.Enabled := (CutGrid1.RowCount > 0)or(CutGrid2.RowCount > 0)or
                            (CutGrid3.RowCount > 0)or(CutGrid4.RowCount > 0)or(CutGrid5.RowCount > 0);
    CutPrintBtn.Enabled  := (CutGrid1.RowCount > 0)or(CutGrid2.RowCount > 0)or
                            (CutGrid3.RowCount > 0)or(CutGrid4.RowCount > 0)or(CutGrid5.RowCount > 0);
  end;
  ChkLook.Enabled := Not ChkEdit.Checked;
  PrintCustBtn.Enabled := InputGrid.RowCount > 0;
end;

  end;
end;

procedure TfMain.TabNumSpecShow(Sender: TObject);
VAr QrLimitNum: TABSQuery;
    NumLen,i: Integer;
    Num: String;
    Pr1,Pr2,Pr3,Pr4,Pr5,Pr6,Pr7: Real;
begin
  Application.ProcessMessages;
  with dm do
  begin
    Limit3.Close;
    Limit3.ParamByName('LNum').Value   := PageControlLimit.TabIndex+1;
    Limit3.ParamByName('aDate').Value  := DateToStr(DatePick.Date);
    Limit3.ParamByName('aLotID').Value := StrToInt(edLotID.Text);
    Limit3.Open;
  end;
end;

begin
 DateSpecNum := DatePick.Date;
{
 MainDate := DatePick.Date;
 Case MainPageControl.TabIndex of
  2:ShowCorNum;
  3:begin

      with dm do
      begin
        Limit3.Close;

        Limit3.ParamByName('aLotID').Value := StrToInt(edLotID.Text);

      end;
     
    end;
  end;
}

end;

function TfMain.findDealer(Code: String): Boolean;
Var QrFindDealer : TABSQuery;
    i,j: integer;
begin
 with Dm do
 begin
  Result := false;
  QrFindDealer := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
  QrFindDealer.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
  QrFindDealer.Close;
  QrFindDealer.SQL.Clear;
  if EditDealer.Text <> '' then
    QrFindDealer.SQL.Add('Select * from Dealer Where Code like "%'+Code+'%"')
  else
    QrFindDealer.SQL.Add('Select * from Dealer Where Code = "'+Code+'"');// :aCode');

  QrFindDealer.Open;

  If QrFindDealer.Eof then
  begin
    PanelCustName.Caption := '';
    Showmessage('รหัสเจ้ามือไม่ถูกต้อง กรุณากรอกรหัสเจ้ามือใหม่ให้ถูกต้อง');
    for i:= 0 to PayGrid.Rowcount-1 do
    begin
      for j := 0 to PayGrid.ColCount-1 do
      begin
          PayGrid.Cells[j,i] := '0';
      end;
    end;
    EditDealer.SetFocus;
    EditDealer.SelectAll;
    Exit;
  end;
  PayGrid[0,0]   := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('ComRnUp').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,1]   := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('ComRnDwn').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,2]   := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('ComPosUp').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,3]   := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('ComPosDwn').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,4]   := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Com2Up').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,5]   := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Com2Tod').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,6]   := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Com2Dwn').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,7]   := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Com2Mee').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,8]   := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Com2Pos').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,9]   := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Com3Up').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,10]  := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Com3Tod').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,11]  := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Com3Dwn').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,12]  := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Com4').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,13]  := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Com4Tod').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,14]  := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Com5').AsString;
  PayGrid[0,15]  := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Com5Tod').AsString;

  PayGrid[1,0]   := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('PayRnUp').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,1]   := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('PayRnDwn').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,2]   := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('PayPosUp').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,3]   := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('PayPosDwn').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,4]   := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Pay2Up').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,5]   := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Pay2Tod').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,6]   := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Pay2Dwn').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,7]   := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Pay2Mee').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,8]   := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Pay2Pos').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,9]   := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Pay3Up').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,10]  := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Pay3Tod').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,11]  := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Pay3Dwn').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,12]  := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Pay4').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,13]  := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Pay4Tod').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,14]  := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Pay5').AsFloat);
  PayGrid[1,15]  := FormatFloat('###,##0.##',QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Pay5Tod').AsFloat);

  //DealerHasCom := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('HasCom').AsBoolean;
  EditDealer.Text := QrFindDealer.FieldByName('Code').AsString;
  PnDealerName.Caption := ' '+QrFindDealer.FieldByName('FName').AsString+' '+QrFindDealer.FieldByName('LName').AsString;
  EditDealer.SetFocus;
  EditDealer.SelectAll;
  Result := true;
 end;
end;

procedure TfMain.TabByCustRepShow(Sender: TObject);
var QrFindCust : TABSQuery;
    SortBySQL: String;
    i: integer;
begin
 with Dm, CustListRep do
 begin
   Items.Clear;
   Items.BeginUpdate;
   Application.ProcessMessages;

  SortBySQL := ' ORDER BY CustID ASC';

  QrFindCust := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
  QrFindCust.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
  QrFindCust.Close;
  QrFindCust.SQL.Clear;
  QrFindCust.SQL.Add('Select * from Cust '+SortBySQL);
  QrFindCust.Open;

      i := 0;
      QrFindCust.First;
      While Not QrfindCust.Eof do
      begin
         with Items.Add do
         begin
            try
                  if QrFindCust.FieldByName('bfName').AsString = 'นาย' then
                    Items[i].ImageIndex := 0
                  else if QrFindCust.FieldByName('bfName').AsString = 'นาง' then
                    Items[i].ImageIndex := 1
                  else if (QrFindCust.FieldByName('bfName').AsString = 'ด.ช.')or(QrFindCust.FieldByName('bfName').AsString = 'ด.ญ.') then
                    Items[i].ImageIndex := 2
                  else if QrFindCust.FieldByName('bfName').AsString = 'นางสาว' then
                    Items[i].ImageIndex := 3
                  else if QrFindCust.FieldByName('bfName').AsString = '' then
                    Items[i].ImageIndex := 4;

               Caption := QrfindCust.FieldByName('CustID').AsString;// findfileData.cFileName;
               SubItems.Add(QrFindCust.fieldByName('FName').AsString+' '+QrFindCust.fieldByName('LName').AsString);
               QrFindCust.Next;
               inc(i);
            except
            end;
         end;
      end;
    Items.EndUpdate;
    QrFindCust.Free;
    CustListRep.SetFocus;
 end;
end;

procedure TfMain.BtnSaveLotNumClick(Sender: TObject);
var QrUpDateCorNum,QrFindCorNum : TABSQuery;
    year,month,day: Word;
    aDate,bDate: TDateTime;
    i: integer;
begin
  with Dm do
  begin
        QrUpDateCorNum := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
        QrUpDateCorNum.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
        QrUpDateCorNum.Close;
        QrUpDateCorNum.SQL.Clear;

        Try
          if Not foundCorNum then // สถานะเพิ่ม
          begin
            QrUpDateCorNum.SQL.Add('INSERT INTO CorrectNum (CrDate, LotType,Num2,Num31,Num32,Num33,Num34,Num6)');
            QrUpDateCorNum.SQL.Add('VALUES (:aCrDate, :aLotType, :aNum2, :aNum31, :aNum32, :aNum33, :aNum34, :aNum6)');

            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aCrDate').Value  := DateToStr(DatePick.Date);
            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aLotType').Value := StrToInt(edLotID.Text);
            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aNum2').Value    := LotNum2.text;
            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aNum31').Value   := LotNum31.text;
            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aNum32').Value   := LotNum32.text;
            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aNum33').Value   := LotNum33.text;
            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aNum34').Value   := LotNum34.text;
            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aNum6').Value    := LotNum6.text;
            QrUpDateCorNum.ExecSQL;
            Showmessage('ข้อมูลได้รับการบันทึกเพิ่มเรียบร้อยแล้ว');
          end
          else
          begin
            QrUpDateCorNum.SQL.Add('UpDate CorrectNum');
            QrUpDateCorNum.SQL.Add('Set Num2=:aNum2, Num31=:aNum31, Num32=:aNum32, Num33=:aNum33, Num34=:aNum34, Num6=:aNum6');
            QrUpDateCorNum.SQL.Add('Where (CrDate = :aCrDate) and (LotType = :aLotType)');

            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aCrDate').Value    := DateTostr(DatePick.Date);
            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aLotType').Value   := StrToInt(edLotID.Text);
            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aNum2').Value    := LotNum2.text;
            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aNum31').Value   := LotNum31.text;
            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aNum32').Value   := LotNum32.text;
            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aNum33').Value   := LotNum33.text;
            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aNum34').Value   := LotNum34.text;
            QrUpDateCorNum.ParamByName('aNum6').Value    := LotNum6.text;
            QrUpDateCorNum.ExecSQL;
            Showmessage('ข้อมูลได้รับการบันทึกแก้ไขเรียบร้อยแล้ว');
          end;

        except
          Showmessage('เกิดปัญหาในการบันทึกข้อมูล ไม่สามารถบันทึกข้อมูลได้');
        end;
        QrFindCorNum.Free;
        LotNum6.SetFocus;
  end;

end;

procedure TfMain.TabUsersShow(Sender: TObject);
var QrUser : TABSQuery;
    SortBySQL: String;
    i: integer;
begin
  Application.ProcessMessages;
  with Dm, UserListRep do
  begin
    SortBySQL := ' ORDER BY UsID ASC';
    QrUser := TABSQuery.Create(nil);
    QrUser.DatabaseName := Database.DatabaseName;
    QrUser.Close;
    QrUser.SQL.Clear;
    QrUser.SQL.Add('Select * from Users '+SortBySQL);
    QrUser.Open;

    QrUser.First;
    Items.Clear;
    Items.BeginUpdate;
    While Not QrUser.Eof do
    begin
      with Items.Add do
      begin
          Caption := QrUser.FieldByName('UsID').AsString;// findfileData.cFileName;
          SubItems.Add(QrUser.fieldByName('Name').AsString);
          QrUser.Next;
      end;
    end;
    Items.EndUpdate;
    QrUser.Free;
    UserListRep.SetFocus;
 end;
end;
